[[sec-2-jtag2axi-write-scenarios]]
==== 2. JTAG2AXI Write Scenarios

NOTE: Every JTAG2AXI scenario in this section and in <<sec-3-jtag2axi-read-scenarios>> and
<<sec-4-jtag2axi-error-backpressure-cdc-and-decode-scenarios>> opens each pass with the geometry gate of the JTAG2AXI base
sequence: it reads the three `*_JTAG2AXI_CAPS` TDRs and records `CHK-J2A-GEOMETRY` for the bus type,
address size, and data size of each bridge against the DV geometry table. A DUT that publishes a
different geometry fails the pass before any bridge
request is packed with the table's field widths.

[[sec-20-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_wr_test]]
===== 2.0 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_wr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: Legacy module-name enrolment of the `single_write` scenario, run as a loop.

*Test Coverage*:

* `testlists/jtag2axi.toml` enrols the module name itself, tagged `legacy`, alongside the
  per-scenario entries. It passes no `args`, so the body runs its default scenario --
  `single_write` -- which is the scenario <<sec-21-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_test,2.1>> cards.
* *This entry adds no behavioural claim of its own.* What `single_write` proves is
  stated once, in <<sec-21-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_test,`dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_test`>>, and is deliberately not restated
  here so the two cannot drift apart.
* What the enrolment does add is repetition and an activity floor: the body loops the
  scenario 16 times by default (`DTP_JTAG2AXI_SMC_AXI_WR_TEST_LOOPS`, else the group knob
  `DTP_JTAG2AXI_TEST_LOOPS`) and asserts `DtpJtag2AxiStatus.SUCCESS` on every iteration.
* The shared AXI checker is enabled (`use_axi_scoreboard = True`); the body requires the
  evidence IDs `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-STRB`, `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` and a minimum of 2 compared transactions on the `smc_axi` stream, so
  a run that drove nothing fails at finalization instead of passing silently.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Resolve the loop count from `DTP_JTAG2AXI_SMC_AXI_WR_TEST_LOOPS`, else `DTP_JTAG2AXI_TEST_LOOPS`, else 16.
. Run the `single_write` scenario that many times through `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_wr_test_seq`; the per-iteration
  steps are the ones in <<sec-21-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_test,2.1>>.
. Assert every iteration returned `DtpJtag2AxiStatus.SUCCESS`.
. At finalization, require the shared AXI checker to have recorded all four evidence IDs
  and at least 2 compared transactions on `smc_axi`.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-AXI-RESP` |Shared AXI checker: every observed response matches the reference model
|`CHK-AXI-STRB` |Shared AXI checker: write strobes match the reference model
|`CHK-AXI-COMPLETION` |Shared AXI checker: no transaction is left outstanding at end of test
|`CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` |At least 2 transactions were compared on the `smc_axi` stream, so a no-op run cannot pass
|`single_write_status_chk` |Every loop iteration returned `DtpJtag2AxiStatus.SUCCESS`
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every iteration of `single_write` returns SUCCESS, all four shared-AXI
evidence IDs are recorded, and at least 2 `smc_axi` transactions were compared, with zero
scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-21-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_test]]
===== 2.1 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric single write

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op AXI4 write through the SMC fabric debug bridge (SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP, IR=0x28)
* 56-bit address / 64-bit data field formatting consistent with the SMC fabric CAPS TDR (IR=0x27, ADDR_SIZE=56, DATA_SIZE=64)
* Address/data/wstrb delivery to the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` / `axi_smc_dbg_resp_i`
* Op-status transitions through Busy (0b11) and settles at OKAY (0b00) after the write completes

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load and verify the bridge CAPS:
a. Load SMC_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x27) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
b. Verify bus_type = AXI4, ADDR_SIZE = 56, DATA_SIZE = 64.
. Issue the single write:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28).
b. Shift in the 56-bit target address, 64-bit write data, wstrb = all-ones, size, and op = 0b10 (Write); then Update-DR.
. Poll for completion:
a. Re-enter Capture-DR/Shift-DR and read the op-status field.
b. Repeat until status is no longer Busy (0b11).
c. Verify op = 0b00 (OKAY).
. Verify the side effect:
a. Confirm the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate received exactly one AW/W beat at the target address with the expected data and wstrb.
b. Confirm the captured B-response is OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_wr_caps_chk` |CAPS TDR reports bus_type=AXI4, ADDR_SIZE=56, DATA_SIZE=64
|`smc_axi_wr_status_chk` |Op-status passes through Busy then returns OKAY (0b00)
|`smc_axi_wr_data_chk` |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate holds the written address/data/wstrb
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The AXI4 single write completes with OKAY status and the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate captures the correct address, data, and wstrb, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-21a-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_data_verify_test]]
===== 2.1a `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_data_verify_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric single-write data verification through read-back

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC fabric single write delivers the expected data bytes, not only status/address fields
* Read-back through the same bridge verifies final memory-visible data and lane placement
* Write status remains OKAY and no unexpected AXI sideband error is observed

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Issue a SMC fabric single write with a non-trivial data pattern and full byte enables.
. Poll the op-status TDR until it returns OKAY.
. Issue a read to the same address through the SMC fabric bridge.
. Compare returned data against the written pattern.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_wr_data_readback_chk` |Read-back data equals the written data pattern
|`smc_axi_wr_status_okay_chk` |Write and read statuses both settle to OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The written data is observable through bridge read-back at the same address with OKAY status and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-22-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_incr_test]]
===== 2.2 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing series write

*Test Coverage*:

* A series of JTAG2AXI AXI4 writes through the SMC fabric bridge with monotonically incrementing addresses (base, base{plus}stride, ...)
* SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) preloading the base address and SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x2A) carrying each beat, the bridge advancing the series address by the beat size after every write
* Distinct data word per address landing at the correct `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` location (no address aliasing)
* Address advance by the 64-bit beat, confirmed by a SERIES_CTRL readback of OKAY status at base{plus}N*stride

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the series parameters:
a. Choose a beat-aligned base address, a stride equal to the 64-bit beat, and a beat count N.
b. Draw a distinct data word per beat and mirror each expected write in a reference memory.
. Issue the incrementing write series:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x2A); Update-DR issues the write at base{plus}i*stride and advances the series address.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write handshake and confirm the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate holds the data word at base{plus}i*stride.
. Verify the series end state:
a. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = base{plus}N*stride.
b. Confirm no writes landed outside the addressed range.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_wr_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) and address = base {plus} N*stride after the series
|`smc_axi_wr_incr_addr_chk` |Captured AW addresses follow base {plus} i*stride with no aliasing
|`smc_axi_wr_incr_data_chk` |Each address holds its distinct data word
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N incrementing writes complete, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at base{plus}N*stride, and each target address holds its data word, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-22a-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_incr_narrow_test]]
===== 2.2a `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_incr_narrow_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric 32-bit incrementing series write at beat offset +4

*Test Coverage*:

* A SERIES_DATA_INCR write stream with `size=2` (32-bit) whose base address is beat-aligned plus four bytes
* AXI WSTRB and WDATA placed on the high 32-bit lanes of the 64-bit SMC fabric beat (`WSTRB=0xF0`, data in `[63:32]`)
* Distinct 32-bit payload per address landing at `base+i*4` in the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate (no low-lane aliasing)
* Address advance of four bytes per beat, consistent with `AxSIZE=2`

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the series parameters:
a. Choose a beat-aligned base, add four, set stride to four bytes, and choose a beat count N.
b. Build a per-beat 32-bit data pattern and mirror each expected write in a reference memory at the size-aligned address.
. Issue the incrementing write series:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with `op=Write`, `size=2`, and the +4 base; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th 32-bit payload through SERIES_DATA_INCR; Update-DR.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the corresponding AXI write handshake and confirm the responder holds the payload at `base+i*4`.
. Verify SERIES_CTRL capture:
a. Confirm status is OKAY and the captured address equals `base+N*4`.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_wr_incr_narrow_status_chk` |Every beat returns OKAY after Busy
|`smc_axi_wr_incr_narrow_lane_chk` |Each write uses the AXI byte lanes selected by the current address (`WSTRB=0xF0` at +4)
|`smc_axi_wr_incr_narrow_data_chk` |Each size-aligned address holds its distinct 32-bit payload
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N 32-bit incrementing writes complete with OKAY status, land on the address-selected AXI lanes, and each target address holds its expected data, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-23-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_no_incr_test]]
===== 2.3 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_no_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric fixed-address series write

*Test Coverage*:

* A series of JTAG2AXI AXI4 writes through the SMC fabric bridge all targeting one fixed address (no-incr mode)
* SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) holding one address while SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x2B) carries every beat without advancing it
* Each beat reuses the same address with fresh data, exercising write-over-write at one `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` location
* OKAY status and an unchanged address in the SERIES_CTRL readback after the series; final memory content equals the last data beat written

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the fixed-address series:
a. Choose a single beat-aligned target address and draw N distinct data words.
b. Track the last-written value as the expected final state in a reference memory.
. Issue the fixed-address write series:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x2B); Update-DR issues the write and leaves the series address unchanged.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write handshake and confirm the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate holds the i-th data word at the target address.
. Verify the single location:
a. Confirm all N writes targeted exactly the one fixed address on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`.
b. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = the target address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_wr_no_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) and an unchanged address after the series
|`smc_axi_wr_no_incr_addr_chk` |All captured AW addresses equal the single fixed address
|`smc_axi_wr_no_incr_data_chk` |Fixed location holds the final (last-beat) data
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N fixed-address writes complete, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at the unchanged address, and the single target location holds the last data beat, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-24-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 2.4 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_incr_with_error_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric series write with status

*Test Coverage*:

* An incrementing AXI4 write series through the SMC fabric bridge carried by SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x2C), the series-data form whose shift selects the address increment per beat and returns the previous beat's error status
* The increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1: the second beat re-writes the held address, so the series spans three addresses and the later word wins at the held one
* One beat, chosen at random among the beats that first touch their address, returns SLVERR or DECERR; the status bit is 1 on the shift after that beat and 0 on every other shift, including a trailing shift that returns the last beat's status
* The faulting beat leaves its slot unchanged while every other beat lands, so a held-address re-write after a faulting first write lands
* SERIES_CTRL readback reporting the pattern's final address, then a legal SINGLE_OP write outside the stream completing OKAY
* The error-series entry <<sec-47-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test,4.7>> covers the same series-data form on an incrementing-only stream together with the SERIES_CTRL fault code

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address whose four-slot footprint fits the target window, draw a data word per beat of the increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1, and pick the fault beat and its response (SLVERR or DECERR) from the scenario RNG.
. Arm the responder to return that response once at the fault beat's address and record the slot's prior word.
. Issue the series through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat: shift the data word and the beat's increment flag through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x2C); Update-DR issues the write and advances the series address only when the flag is set.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write handshake, verify the returned status bit is 1 only on the shift after the fault beat, and confirm the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate holds the data word at the beat's address, or the prior word at the faulting beat's address.
d. Shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) and verify its status bit reports the last beat.
. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride, the pattern's final address.
. Issue a legal SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) write to a slot outside the stream; verify OKAY and the memory update, proving recovery.
. Negative validation: `DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE=1` (cocotb) or `+DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE` (SV-UVM) keeps the expectation but leaves the fault unarmed, so the fault beat's checks must fail the run.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_wr_status_bit_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: the status bit is 1 on the shift after the fault beat and 0 on every other shift, trailing shift included
|`smc_axi_wr_status_data_chk` |Each non-faulting beat's address holds the last data word written to it; the faulting beat's slot keeps its prior word
|`smc_axi_wr_status_addr_chk` |SERIES_CTRL address readback equals base {plus} 3*stride (the held beat did not advance the address)
|`smc_axi_wr_status_recover_chk` |A legal single write outside the stream completes OKAY with its memory update; the armed response reaches the bus and is consumed (`CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ` SV-UVM)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The fault beat's response surfaces on the following shift's status bit and nowhere else, every other beat lands, the faulting slot is unchanged, the SERIES_CTRL readback shows base{plus}3*stride, and the recovery write completes OKAY, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-25-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_random_ops_test]]
===== 2.5 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_random_ops_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric randomized write operations

*Test Coverage*:

* Constrained-random JTAG2AXI AXI4 writes through the SMC fabric bridge over randomized address, data, wstrb, and size
* Stress of the 56-bit address / 64-bit data field packing across the SINGLE_OP TDR
* A reference memory mirroring every issued write, compared against the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate at the end
* Op-status consistency (OKAY for in-range writes) across a long randomized sequence

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Generate the randomized workload:
a. Build a constrained-random set of write operations (random in-range address, random data, random legal wstrb, random size) for the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
b. Apply each accepted write to a reference memory model.
. Issue each operation:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28).
b. For each randomized op: shift in address, data, wstrb, size, op = 0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy (0b11) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY).
. Compare end state:
a. Read back / inspect the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate and compare every written location against the reference memory.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_rand_status_chk` |Every randomized write returns OKAY (0b00)
|`smc_axi_rand_mem_chk` |Final subordinate image matches the reference memory exactly
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized writes complete with OKAY status and the final subordinate memory image matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-26-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_write_security_gating_test]]
===== 2.6 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_write_security_gating_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric write security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1` disables the SMC fabric bridge; deriving the bit from lifecycle policy is SEP-level verification
* When gated, the SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP TDR (IR=0x28) remains selected while Update-DR requests are suppressed: no AXI traffic appears on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` and a preloaded RAM sentinel at the gated address stays unchanged, both while gated and after the disable is released (no delayed replay)
* Two assert/release passes of the same disable prove the gate is repeatable, not a one-shot power-on effect
* A legal write succeeds before gating and again after each release (clean re-enable)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Baseline write (un-gated):
a. With all disables clear, issue one SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP write (IR=0x28, op=0b10) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY) and the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate captured it.
. Gate (per pass, twice):
a. Assert `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1` and settle four toggling TCK cycles.
b. Preload a RAM sentinel at the gated address, load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28), and shift a write request.
c. Verify no AW/W transaction is issued on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` and the sentinel is unchanged.
d. Clear the disable without reset; verify the sentinel is still unchanged (no delayed replay), then verify a legal write again completes with OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_gate_no_traffic_chk` |No AXI write appears on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` while gated
|`smc_axi_gate_sentinel_chk` |The preloaded RAM sentinel is unchanged while gated and after release (no delayed replay)
|`smc_axi_gate_restore_chk` |A legal write completes with OKAY after each release
|===

*Pass Criteria*: With `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi` asserted the write is suppressed with no AXI traffic and no memory mutation, and each release restores a passing OKAY write, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-27-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_test]]
===== 2.7 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP single write

*Test Coverage*:

* JTAG2AXI single-op AXI4-Lite write through the SMC OTP bridge (SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP, IR=0x1C)
* 32-bit address / 32-bit data field formatting consistent with the SMC OTP CAPS TDR (IR=0x1B, AXI4-Lite, 32/32)
* Address/data/wstrb delivery to the AXI-Lite subordinate on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`
* Op-status transitions through Busy (0b11) and settles at OKAY (0b00)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load and verify the bridge CAPS:
a. Load SMC_OTP_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x1B) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver` and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
b. Verify bus_type = AXI4-Lite, ADDR_SIZE = 32, DATA_SIZE = 32.
. Issue the single write:
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C).
b. Shift in the 32-bit target address, 32-bit write data, wstrb = all-ones, op = 0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
. Poll for completion:
a. Re-enter Capture-DR/Shift-DR and read the op-status field until not Busy (0b11).
b. Verify op = 0b00 (OKAY).
. Verify the side effect:
a. Confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate received the write at the target address with the expected data and wstrb.
b. Confirm the captured B-response is OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_caps_chk` |CAPS TDR reports bus_type=AXI4-Lite, 32-bit address/data
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_status_chk` |Op-status passes through Busy then returns OKAY (0b00)
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_data_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate holds the written address/data/wstrb
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The AXI4-Lite single write completes with OKAY status and the SMC OTP subordinate captures the correct address and data, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-27a-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_data_verify_test]]
===== 2.7a `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_data_verify_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP single-write data verification through read-back

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC OTP AXI-Lite single write delivers the expected 32-bit data word
* Read-back verifies byte-lane placement and final memory-visible value
* Write/read operations both return OKAY status

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Issue a SMC OTP AXI-Lite single write with a non-zero 32-bit pattern and valid write strobes.
. Poll until write status is OKAY.
. Read the same SMC OTP address through the bridge.
. Compare the returned word against the written pattern.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_wr_data_readback_chk` |Read-back data equals the written SMC OTP word
|`smc_otp_wr_status_okay_chk` |Write and read statuses both settle to OKAY
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The SMC OTP written word is returned by bridge read-back with OKAY status and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-28-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_incr_test]]
===== 2.8 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP incrementing series write

*Test Coverage*:

* A series of JTAG2AXI AXI4-Lite writes through the SMC OTP bridge with monotonically incrementing addresses (base, base{plus}stride, ...)
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) preloading the base address and SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E) carrying each beat, the bridge advancing the series address by the beat size after every write
* Distinct data word per address landing at the correct AXI-Lite subordinate location (no address aliasing)
* Address advance by the 32-bit beat, confirmed by a SERIES_CTRL readback of OKAY status at base{plus}N*stride

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the series parameters:
a. Choose a beat-aligned base address, a stride equal to the 32-bit beat, and a beat count N.
b. Draw a distinct data word per beat and mirror each expected write in a reference memory.
. Issue the incrementing write series:
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E); Update-DR issues the write at base{plus}i*stride and advances the series address.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the data word at base{plus}i*stride.
. Verify the series end state:
a. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = base{plus}N*stride.
b. Confirm no writes landed outside the addressed range.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) and address = base {plus} N*stride after the series
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_incr_addr_chk` |Captured AW addresses follow base {plus} i*stride with no aliasing
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_incr_data_chk` |Each address holds its distinct data word
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N incrementing writes complete, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at base{plus}N*stride, and each target address holds its data word, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-29-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_no_incr_test]]
===== 2.9 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_no_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP fixed-address series write

*Test Coverage*:

* A series of JTAG2AXI AXI4-Lite writes through the SMC OTP bridge all targeting one fixed 32-bit address (no-incr mode)
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) holding one address while SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x1F) carries every beat without advancing it
* Each beat reuses the same address with fresh data, exercising write-over-write at one AXI-Lite subordinate location
* OKAY status and an unchanged address in the SERIES_CTRL readback after the series; final memory content equals the last data beat written

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the fixed-address series:
a. Choose a single beat-aligned target address and draw N distinct data words.
b. Track the last-written value as the expected final state in a reference memory.
. Issue the fixed-address write series:
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x1F); Update-DR issues the write and leaves the series address unchanged.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the i-th data word at the target address.
. Verify the single location:
a. Confirm all N writes targeted exactly the one fixed address on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o`.
b. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = the target address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) and an unchanged address after the series
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_addr_chk` |All captured AW addresses equal the single fixed address
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_data_chk` |Fixed location holds the final (last-beat) data
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N fixed-address writes complete, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at the unchanged address, and the single target location holds the last data beat, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-210-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 2.10 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_incr_with_error_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP series write with status

*Test Coverage*:

* An incrementing AXI4-Lite write series through the SMC OTP bridge carried by SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x20), the series-data form whose shift selects the address increment per beat and returns the previous beat's error status
* The increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1: the second beat re-writes the held address, so the series spans three addresses and the later word wins at the held one
* One beat, chosen at random among the beats that first touch their address, returns SLVERR or DECERR; the status bit is 1 on the shift after that beat and 0 on every other shift, including a trailing shift that returns the last beat's status
* The faulting beat leaves its slot unchanged while every other beat lands, so a held-address re-write after a faulting first write lands
* SERIES_CTRL readback reporting the pattern's final address, then a legal SINGLE_OP write outside the stream completing OKAY
* The error-series entry <<sec-416-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test,4.16>> covers the same series-data form on an incrementing-only stream together with the SERIES_CTRL fault code

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address whose four-slot footprint fits the target window, draw a data word per beat of the increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1, and pick the fault beat and its response (SLVERR or DECERR) from the scenario RNG.
. Arm the responder to return that response once at the fault beat's address and record the slot's prior word.
. Issue the series through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS:
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat: shift the data word and the beat's increment flag through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x20); Update-DR issues the write and advances the series address only when the flag is set.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake, verify the returned status bit is 1 only on the shift after the fault beat, and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the data word at the beat's address, or the prior word at the faulting beat's address.
d. Shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) and verify its status bit reports the last beat.
. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride, the pattern's final address.
. Issue a legal SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) write to a slot outside the stream; verify OKAY and the memory update, proving recovery.
. Negative validation: `DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE=1` (cocotb) or `+DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE` (SV-UVM) keeps the expectation but leaves the fault unarmed, so the fault beat's checks must fail the run.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_status_bit_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: the status bit is 1 on the shift after the fault beat and 0 on every other shift, trailing shift included
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_status_data_chk` |Each non-faulting beat's address holds the last data word written to it; the faulting beat's slot keeps its prior word
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_status_addr_chk` |SERIES_CTRL address readback equals base {plus} 3*stride (the held beat did not advance the address)
|`smc_otp_axi_wr_status_recover_chk` |A legal single write outside the stream completes OKAY with its memory update; the armed response reaches the bus and is consumed (`CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ` SV-UVM)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The fault beat's response surfaces on the following shift's status bit and nowhere else, every other beat lands, the faulting slot is unchanged, the SERIES_CTRL readback shows base{plus}3*stride, and the recovery write completes OKAY, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-211-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_random_ops_test]]

b. Apply each accepted write to a reference memory model.

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized writes complete with OKAY status and the final subordinate memory image matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-212-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_write_security_gating_test]]
===== 2.12 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_write_security_gating_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `dbg_disable_i.smc_otp_jtag2axi = 1` disables the SMC OTP bridge; deriving the bit from lifecycle policy is SEP-level verification
* When gated, the SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP TDR (IR=0x1C) remains selected while Update-DR requests are suppressed: no AXI-Lite traffic appears on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` and a preloaded RAM sentinel at the gated address stays unchanged, both while gated and after the disable is released (no delayed replay)
* Two assert/release passes of the same disable prove the gate is repeatable
* A legal write succeeds before gating and again after each release (clean re-enable)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Baseline write (un-gated):
a. With all disables clear, issue one SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP write (IR=0x1C, op=0b10) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY) and the AXI-Lite subordinate captured it.
. Gate (per pass, twice):
a. Assert `dbg_disable_i.smc_otp_jtag2axi = 1`, settle four toggling TCK cycles, preload a RAM sentinel, load IR=0x1C, and shift a write request; verify no transaction on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` and the sentinel unchanged.
b. Clear the disable without reset; verify the sentinel is still unchanged (no delayed replay), then verify normal operation resumes.
. Final restore check:
a. With all required enables high, verify a legal write again completes with OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_axi_gate_no_traffic_chk` |No AXI-Lite write appears on `axil_smc_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated
|`smc_otp_axi_gate_sentinel_chk` |The preloaded RAM sentinel is unchanged while gated and after release (no delayed replay)
|`smc_otp_axi_gate_restore_chk` |A legal write completes with OKAY after each release
|===

*Pass Criteria*: With `dbg_disable_i.smc_otp_jtag2axi` asserted the write is suppressed with no AXI-Lite traffic and no memory mutation, and each release restores a passing OKAY write, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-214-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_incr_test]]
===== 2.14 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP incrementing series write

*Test Coverage*:

* A series of JTAG2AXI AXI4-Lite writes through the SEP OTP bridge with monotonically incrementing addresses (base, base{plus}stride, ...)
* SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) preloading the base address and SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x24) carrying each beat, the bridge advancing the series address by the beat size after every write
* Distinct data word per address landing at the correct AXI-Lite subordinate location (no address aliasing)
* Address advance by the 32-bit beat, confirmed by a SERIES_CTRL readback of OKAY status at base{plus}N*stride

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the series parameters:
a. Choose a beat-aligned base address, a stride equal to the 32-bit beat, and a beat count N.
b. Draw a distinct data word per beat and mirror each expected write in a reference memory.
. Issue the incrementing write series:
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x24); Update-DR issues the write at base{plus}i*stride and advances the series address.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the data word at base{plus}i*stride.
. Verify the series end state:
a. Capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = base{plus}N*stride.
b. Confirm no writes landed outside the addressed range.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) and address = base {plus} N*stride after the series
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_incr_addr_chk` |Captured AW addresses follow base {plus} i*stride with no aliasing
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_incr_data_chk` |Each address holds its distinct data word
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N incrementing writes complete, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at base{plus}N*stride, and each target address holds its data word, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-215-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_no_incr_test]]
===== 2.15 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_no_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP fixed-address series write

*Test Coverage*:

* A series of JTAG2AXI AXI4-Lite writes through the SEP OTP bridge all targeting one fixed 32-bit address (no-incr mode)
* SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) holding one address while SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x25) carries every beat without advancing it
* Each beat reuses the same address with fresh data, exercising write-over-write at one AXI-Lite subordinate location
* OKAY status and an unchanged address in the SERIES_CTRL readback after the series; final memory content equals the last data beat written

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish the fixed-address series:
a. Choose a single beat-aligned target address and draw N distinct data words.
b. Track the last-written value as the expected final state in a reference memory.
. Issue the fixed-address write series:
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x25); Update-DR issues the write and leaves the series address unchanged.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the i-th data word at the target address.
. Verify the single location:
a. Confirm all N writes targeted exactly the one fixed address on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o`.
b. Capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = the target address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) and an unchanged address after the series
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_addr_chk` |All captured AW addresses equal the single fixed address
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_no_incr_data_chk` |Fixed location holds the final (last-beat) data
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All N fixed-address writes complete, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at the unchanged address, and the single target location holds the last data beat, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-216-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 2.16 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_series_write_incr_with_error_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP series write with status

*Test Coverage*:

* An incrementing AXI4-Lite write series through the SEP OTP bridge carried by SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x26), the series-data form whose shift selects the address increment per beat and returns the previous beat's error status
* The increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1: the second beat re-writes the held address, so the series spans three addresses and the later word wins at the held one
* One beat, chosen at random among the beats that first touch their address, returns SLVERR or DECERR; the status bit is 1 on the shift after that beat and 0 on every other shift, including a trailing shift that returns the last beat's status
* The faulting beat leaves its slot unchanged while every other beat lands, so a held-address re-write after a faulting first write lands
* SERIES_CTRL readback reporting the pattern's final address, then a legal SINGLE_OP write outside the stream completing OKAY
* The error-series entry <<sec-425-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test,4.25>> covers the same series-data form on an incrementing-only stream together with the SERIES_CTRL fault code

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address whose four-slot footprint fits the target window, draw a data word per beat of the increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1, and pick the fault beat and its response (SLVERR or DECERR) from the scenario RNG.
. Arm the responder to return that response once at the fault beat's address and record the slot's prior word.
. Issue the series through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS:
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x23) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat: shift the data word and the beat's increment flag through SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x26); Update-DR issues the write and advances the series address only when the flag is set.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake, verify the returned status bit is 1 only on the shift after the fault beat, and confirm the AXI-Lite subordinate holds the data word at the beat's address, or the prior word at the faulting beat's address.
d. Shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) and verify its status bit reports the last beat.
. Capture SEP_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride, the pattern's final address.
. Issue a legal SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22) write to a slot outside the stream; verify OKAY and the memory update, proving recovery.
. Negative validation: `DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE=1` (cocotb) or `+DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE` (SV-UVM) keeps the expectation but leaves the fault unarmed, so the fault beat's checks must fail the run.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_status_bit_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: the status bit is 1 on the shift after the fault beat and 0 on every other shift, trailing shift included
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_status_data_chk` |Each non-faulting beat's address holds the last data word written to it; the faulting beat's slot keeps its prior word
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_status_addr_chk` |SERIES_CTRL address readback equals base {plus} 3*stride (the held beat did not advance the address)
|`sep_otp_axi_wr_status_recover_chk` |A legal single write outside the stream completes OKAY with its memory update; the armed response reaches the bus and is consumed (`CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ` SV-UVM)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The fault beat's response surfaces on the following shift's status bit and nowhere else, every other beat lands, the faulting slot is unchanged, the SERIES_CTRL readback shows base{plus}3*stride, and the recovery write completes OKAY, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-217-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_random_ops_test]]
===== 2.17 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_random_ops_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP randomized write operations

*Test Coverage*:

* Constrained-random JTAG2AXI AXI4-Lite writes through the SEP OTP bridge over randomized address, data, and wstrb
* Stress of the 32-bit address / 32-bit data field packing across the SINGLE_OP TDR
* A reference memory mirroring every issued write, compared against the AXI-Lite subordinate at the end
* Op-status consistency (OKAY for in-range writes) across a long randomized sequence

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Generate the randomized workload:
a. Build a constrained-random set of write operations (random in-range 32-bit address, random data, random legal wstrb) for the SEP OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
b. Apply each accepted write to a reference memory model.
. Issue each operation:
a. Load SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x22).
b. For each randomized op: shift in address, data, wstrb, op = 0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll Capture-DR/Shift-DR until not Busy (0b11) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY).
. Compare end state:
a. Inspect the AXI-Lite subordinate and compare every written location against the reference memory.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_rand_status_chk` |Every randomized write returns OKAY (0b00)
|`sep_otp_axi_rand_mem_chk` |Final subordinate image matches the reference memory exactly
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized writes complete with OKAY status and the final subordinate memory image matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-218-dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_write_security_gating_test]]
===== 2.18 `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_write_security_gating_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_sep_otp_axi_wr_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SEP OTP write security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `dbg_disable_i.sep_otp_jtag2axi = 1` disables the SEP OTP bridge; deriving the bit from lifecycle policy is SEP-level verification
* When gated, the SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP TDR (IR=0x22) remains selected while Update-DR requests are suppressed: no AXI-Lite traffic appears on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o` and a preloaded RAM sentinel at the gated address stays unchanged, both while gated and after the disable is released (no delayed replay)
* Two assert/release passes of the same disable prove the gate is repeatable
* A legal write succeeds before gating and again after each release (clean re-enable)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Baseline write (un-gated):
a. With all disables clear, issue one SEP_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP write (IR=0x22, op=0b10) and verify op = 0b00 (OKAY) and the AXI-Lite subordinate captured it.
. Gate (per pass, twice):
a. Assert `dbg_disable_i.sep_otp_jtag2axi = 1`, settle four toggling TCK cycles, preload a RAM sentinel, load IR=0x22, and shift a write request; verify no transaction on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o` and the sentinel unchanged.
b. Clear the disable without reset; verify the sentinel is still unchanged (no delayed replay), then verify normal operation resumes.
. Final restore check:
a. With all required enables high, verify a legal write again completes with OKAY.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sep_otp_axi_gate_no_traffic_chk` |No AXI-Lite write appears on `axil_sep_otp_jtag_req_o` while gated
|`sep_otp_axi_gate_sentinel_chk` |The preloaded RAM sentinel is unchanged while gated and after release (no delayed replay)
|`sep_otp_axi_gate_restore_chk` |A legal write completes with OKAY after each release
|===

*Pass Criteria*: With `dbg_disable_i.sep_otp_jtag2axi` asserted the write is suppressed with no AXI-Lite traffic and no memory mutation, and each release restores a passing OKAY write, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-3-jtag2axi-read-scenarios]]
==== 3. JTAG2AXI Read Scenarios

[[sec-30-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test]]
===== 3.0 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: Legacy module-name enrolment of the `single_write_read` scenario, run as a loop.

*Test Coverage*:

* `testlists/jtag2axi.toml` enrols the module name itself, tagged `legacy`, alongside the
  per-scenario entries. It passes no `args`, so the body runs its default scenario --
  `single_write_read` -- which is the scenario <<sec-31-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_read_test,3.1>> cards.
* *This entry adds no behavioural claim of its own.* What `single_write_read` proves is
  stated once, in <<sec-31-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_read_test,`dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_read_test`>>, and is deliberately not restated
  here so the two cannot drift apart.
* What the enrolment does add is repetition and an activity floor: the body loops the
  scenario 16 times by default (`DTP_JTAG2AXI_SMC_AXI_RD_TEST_LOOPS`, else the group knob
  `DTP_JTAG2AXI_TEST_LOOPS`) and asserts `DtpJtag2AxiStatus.SUCCESS` on every iteration.
* The shared AXI checker is enabled (`use_axi_scoreboard = True`); the body requires the
  evidence IDs `CHK-AXI-RESP`, `CHK-AXI-RDATA`, `CHK-AXI-COMPLETION`, `CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` and a minimum of 2 compared transactions on the `smc_axi` stream, so
  a run that drove nothing fails at finalization instead of passing silently.

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Resolve the loop count from `DTP_JTAG2AXI_SMC_AXI_RD_TEST_LOOPS`, else `DTP_JTAG2AXI_TEST_LOOPS`, else 16.
. Run the `single_write_read` scenario that many times through `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test_seq`; the per-iteration
  steps are the ones in <<sec-31-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_read_test,3.1>>.
. Assert every iteration returned `DtpJtag2AxiStatus.SUCCESS`.
. At finalization, require the shared AXI checker to have recorded all four evidence IDs
  and at least 2 compared transactions on `smc_axi`.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`CHK-AXI-RESP` |Shared AXI checker: every observed response matches the reference model
|`CHK-AXI-RDATA` |Shared AXI checker: read data matches the reference model
|`CHK-AXI-COMPLETION` |Shared AXI checker: no transaction is left outstanding at end of test
|`CHK-AXI-STREAM-MIN` |At least 2 transactions were compared on the `smc_axi` stream, so a no-op run cannot pass
|`single_write_read_status_chk` |Every loop iteration returned `DtpJtag2AxiStatus.SUCCESS`
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every iteration of `single_write_read` returns SUCCESS, all four shared-AXI
evidence IDs are recorded, and at least 2 `smc_axi` transactions were compared, with zero
scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-31-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_read_test]]
===== 3.1 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_single_write_read_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric write-read path

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC fabric AXI4 single-operation write followed by a read-back of the same address
* SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) DR field packing: address (56-bit), data, wstrb, size, op
* Read op encoding `op=0b01` with data=0 and wstrb=0, and busy/terminal status polling
* Data integrity between the written word and the value returned by `OcahAxiSlaveAgent`

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SMC_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x27) and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
a. Verify bus_type = AXI4, addr_size = 56, data_size = 64.
. Issue a single write through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge to a chosen target address.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28).
b. Shift in target address, write data, wstrb=all-ones, size, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll via Capture-DR/Shift-DR until op status leaves Busy (0b11); verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Read back the same address through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Reload SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28).
b. Shift in target address, data=0, wstrb=0, size, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
c. Poll op status until not Busy (0b11), then read returned data and status from the DR.
. Compare the returned read word against the previously written value and the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` reference.
. Verify the terminal op status is 0b00 (OKAY) on both the write and the read.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_single_wr_rd_status_chk` |Write and read both reach op=0b00 (OKAY) after leaving Busy
|`smc_axi_single_wr_rd_rdata_chk` |Returned read word equals the written value and the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` model
|`smc_axi_single_wr_rd_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` content at the target address matches the issued write
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The write completes OKAY, the read-back returns the same word with OKAY status, and the memory side effect matches, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-32-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_test]]
===== 3.2 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric incrementing series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Sequential AXI4 accesses through the SMC fabric bridge across a block of incrementing addresses
* SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) preloading the start address and SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x2A) carrying each beat, the bridge advancing the series address by the beat size after every write
* Per-beat read data integrity against the data words written through the bridge
* OKAY terminal status in the SERIES_CTRL readback across the full series with no stuck-Busy condition
* Single-op addressing across many addresses is covered by <<sec-25-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_random_ops_test,2.5>> and <<sec-35-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_random_ops_test,3.5>>; every SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) shift carries its own address, so reissuing it over a contiguous block is not a distinct bridge property

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address, a stride equal to the 64-bit beat, and a beat count N; draw a distinct data word per beat.
. Write the series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x2A); Update-DR issues the write at base{plus}i*stride and advances the series address.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write handshake and confirm `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` holds the data word at base{plus}i*stride.
. Read the series back in the same incrementing order.
a. For each beat i: load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and address base{plus}i*stride; Update-DR.
b. Shift SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Compare every returned beat with the data word written to that address.
. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_series_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) after the write and read series
|`smc_axi_series_incr_rdata_chk` |Each returned beat matches the data word written to its address
|`smc_axi_series_incr_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` content over the block matches the issued write series
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every incrementing-address write and read completes, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY, and all per-beat comparisons match the written data, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-32a-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_narrow_test]]
===== 3.2a `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_narrow_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric 32-bit incrementing series write-read at beat offset +4

*Test Coverage*:

* A SERIES_DATA_INCR write then read stream with `size=2` whose base is beat-aligned plus four bytes
* Write-side AXI lane placement of the 32-bit logical payload onto the high half of the 64-bit beat
* Capture-DR returning that same logical payload extracted from the address-selected AXI RDATA lanes
* Per-beat memory compare at `base+i*4` against the written 32-bit pattern

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base, add four, set stride to four bytes, and choose a beat count N.
. Write the series through SERIES_CTRL(WRITE) + SERIES_DATA_INCR and confirm each `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` location at `base+i*4` holds the 32-bit payload.
. Read the series back in the same order through SERIES_CTRL(READ) + SERIES_DATA_INCR; Capture-DR returns the logical 32-bit payload.
. Compare every returned beat with the written pattern and the responder memory.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_series_incr_narrow_status_chk` |Every op in the series reaches OKAY after Busy clears
|`smc_axi_series_incr_narrow_rdata_chk` |Each Capture-DR beat matches the written 32-bit payload
|`smc_axi_series_incr_narrow_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` content at each size-aligned address matches the issued write
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every 32-bit incrementing write and read completes OKAY, Capture-DR returns the logical payload from the address-selected lanes, and all per-beat comparisons match, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-33-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test]]
===== 3.3 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric fixed-address series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Repeated AXI4 writes and reads through the SMC fabric bridge targeting one fixed address (no address advance)
* SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) holding one address while SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x2B) carries every beat without advancing it
* Returned data stability when the last-written value is read multiple times
* Series-length robustness with no DR/state corruption between repeated beats, and an unchanged address in the SERIES_CTRL readback

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a single beat-aligned target address and draw N distinct data words.
. Write the N words to the fixed address through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. For each word: shift it through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x2B); Update-DR issues the write and leaves the series address unchanged.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write handshake.
. Read the fixed address N times through the bridge.
a. For each iteration: load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. Shift SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Verify every returned beat equals the last word written.
. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = the target address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_series_no_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) at the unchanged address after the series
|`smc_axi_series_no_incr_rdata_chk` |Every returned beat equals the last word written
|`smc_axi_series_no_incr_mem_chk` |`OcahAxiSlaveAgent` at the fixed address holds the last word written across the series
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All fixed-address beats complete, every returned beat equals the last word written, and the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at the unchanged address, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-34-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 3.4 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric write-read with error status

*Test Coverage*:

* AXI4 write then read series through the SMC fabric bridge carried by SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x2C), the series-data form whose shift selects the address increment per beat and returns the previous beat's error status
* The increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1 on both legs: the second beat re-writes the held address, so the series spans three addresses and the later word wins at the held one
* A clean write leg with status bit 0 on every shift, then a read leg from one SERIES_CTRL preload in which one beat, chosen at random among the beats that first touch their address, returns SLVERR or DECERR
* Read-side Capture-DR returning the previous read's word and status bit: the bit is 1 only on the shift after the faulting read, every other beat returns the last word written to its address, and a held-address re-read after a faulting first read returns the word
* SERIES_CTRL readback reporting the pattern's final address after each leg, then a legal SINGLE_OP read outside the stream completing OKAY
* The error-series entries <<sec-47-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_write_with_status_test,4.7>> and <<sec-48-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_error_series_incr_read_with_status_test,4.8>> cover the same series-data form on incrementing-only streams together with the SERIES_CTRL fault code

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address whose four-slot footprint fits the target window and draw a data word per beat of the increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1.
. Write the series through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x29) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat: shift the data word and the beat's increment flag through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x2C); Update-DR issues the write and advances the series address only when the flag is set.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI write handshake, verify the returned status bit is 0, and confirm the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` subordinate holds the data word at the beat's address; shift one trailing word (data 0, increment 0) and verify its status bit is 0.
d. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride.
. Pick the fault beat and its response (SLVERR or DECERR) from the scenario RNG and arm the responder to return that response once at the beat's address.
. Read the series back from one preload:
a. Load SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. Shift four words carrying the beats' increment flags, then one trailing word (increment 0), through SMC_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS; every Update-DR launches a read, and shift k returns the word and status bit of read k-1.
c. Verify the status bit is 1 only on the shift after the fault beat and every non-faulting beat returns the last word written to its address.
d. Capture SMC_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify address = base{plus}3*stride.
. Issue a legal SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) read of a preloaded slot outside the stream; verify OKAY and the word, proving recovery.
. Negative validation: `DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE=1` (cocotb) or `+DTP_J2A_STATUS_BIT_NEGATIVE` (SV-UVM) keeps the expectation but leaves the fault unarmed, so the fault beat's checks must fail the run.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_series_status_bit_chk` |`CHK-J2A-STATUS-BIT`: status bit 0 on every write-leg shift and 1 only on the read-leg shift after the fault beat
|`smc_axi_series_status_rdata_chk` |Each non-faulting read beat returns the last word written to its address
|`smc_axi_series_status_addr_chk` |SERIES_CTRL address readback equals base {plus} 3*stride after each leg (the held beat did not advance the address)
|`smc_axi_series_status_recover_chk` |A legal single read outside the stream returns its word with OKAY; the armed response reaches the bus and is consumed (`CHK-AXI-RESP-EXPECTED` cocotb, `CHK-AXI-ERR-INJ` SV-UVM)
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The write leg completes clean, the read leg's fault surfaces on the following shift's status bit and nowhere else, every other beat returns its word, both SERIES_CTRL readbacks show base{plus}3*stride, and the recovery read completes OKAY, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-35-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_random_ops_test]]
===== 3.5 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_random_ops_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric randomized reads

*Test Coverage*:

* Constrained-random SMC fabric AXI4 read addresses and sizes against a randomized reference
* SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28) field packing under randomized address/size stimulus
* Per-op data integrity across a randomized address space
* Status correctness (OKAY) for all in-range randomized reads

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Preload `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` with a randomized reference image over the SMC fabric read window.
. Generate a constrained-random sequence of in-range read addresses (and legal sizes).
. Issue each randomized read through the SMC fabric AXI4 bridge.
a. Load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28); shift in the random address, data=0, wstrb=0, size, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
b. Poll op status until not Busy (0b11); capture returned data and status.
. Compare each returned word with the `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` reference model at that address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_axi_random_status_chk` |Every randomized read reaches op=0b00 (OKAY) after Busy clears
|`smc_axi_random_rdata_chk` |Each returned word matches the randomized `OcahAxiSlaveAgent` reference
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized reads complete OKAY and every returned word matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-36-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_test]]
===== 3.6 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric read security gating

*Test Coverage*:

* `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1` gates the SMC fabric read bridge
* Gated read requests are suppressed: the TDR remains selected while no AXI traffic appears on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o`, and the request-activity counters hold flat across a blocked window that stays open through the disable's release (no delayed replay)
* Normal read operation restored after the disable clears, without any reset

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a baseline SMC fabric read returning OKAY with expected data from `OcahAxiSlaveAgent`.
. Gate (per pass, twice): assert `dbg_disable_i.smc_jtag2axi = 1`, settle four toggling TCK cycles, load SMC_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x28), and shift a read request.
a. Snapshot the AW/W/AR activity counters before the attempt and verify no AXI transaction issues on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` across the gated window.
. Clear the disable without reset; hold the blocked window across the release and verify no queued request replays, then verify a normal read is restored with an exact activity delta (only the sanctioned restore read).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_rd_gate_noaxi_chk` |No AXI transaction on `axi_smc_dbg_req_o` while gated
|`smc_rd_gate_noreplay_chk` |The blocked window stays clean across the disable's release
|`smc_rd_gate_restore_chk` |Normal read with OKAY status resumes after the bit clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The asserted disable suppresses all AXI activity with no delayed replay after release, and reads resume correctly once the disable clears, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-36a-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_no_axi_activity_test]]
===== 3.6a `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_read_security_gating_no_axi_activity_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC fabric read security gating with explicit no-AXI-activity check

*Test Coverage*:

* `soc_debug` and `ap_debug` gating prevent any SMC fabric read request from reaching AXI
* No `AWVALID` or `ARVALID` activity occurs while the bridge is gated
* Normal read activity resumes after the required enable is restored

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Establish a baseline SMC fabric read and confirm AXI activity is observed.
. Drive one required enable low (`ap_debug`, then `soc_debug`) and attempt a SMC fabric read.
. Monitor AXI request channels for a bounded window and verify no AW/AR valid activity occurs.
. Restore the required enable and verify a normal read again produces AXI activity and OKAY status.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_rd_gate_no_aw_ar_chk` |No AWVALID or ARVALID while the bridge is gated
|`smc_rd_gate_restore_activity_chk` |AXI read activity resumes after gating clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Each required enable independently blocks all SMC fabric AXI read activity while low and normal reads resume after restore, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-37-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_read_test]]
===== 3.7 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_single_write_read_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write-read path

*Test Coverage*:

* SMC OTP AXI4-Lite single-operation write followed by a read-back of the same address
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) DR field packing: 32-bit address, 32-bit data, wstrb, op
* CAPS reporting bus_type = AXI4-Lite, addr_size = 32, data_size = 32
* Data integrity between the written word and the value returned by the AXI-Lite subordinate

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SMC_OTP_JTAG2AXI_CAPS (IR=0x1B) and read the 14-bit CAPS TDR.
a. Verify bus_type = AXI4-Lite (CAPS bit 0 = 1), addr_size = 32, data_size = 32.
. Issue a single write through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge to a chosen 32-bit address.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C).
b. Shift in target address, write data, wstrb=all-ones, op=0b10 (Write); Update-DR.
c. Poll until op status leaves Busy (0b11); verify op=0b00 (OKAY).
. Read back the same address through the SMC OTP AXI-Lite bridge.
a. Reload SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C).
b. Shift in target address, data=0, wstrb=0, op=0b01 (Read); Update-DR.
c. Poll op status until not Busy; read returned data and status from the DR.
. Compare the returned read word against the written value and the AXI-Lite subordinate model.
. Verify the terminal op status is 0b00 (OKAY) on both the write and the read.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_single_wr_rd_status_chk` |Write and read both reach op=0b00 (OKAY) after leaving Busy
|`smc_otp_single_wr_rd_rdata_chk` |Returned read word equals the written value and the AXI-Lite model
|`smc_otp_single_wr_rd_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate content at the target address matches the issued write
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The write completes OKAY, the read-back returns the same word with OKAY status, and the memory side effect matches, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-38-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_test]]
===== 3.8 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP incrementing series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Sequential AXI4-Lite accesses through the SMC OTP bridge across a block of incrementing 32-bit addresses
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) preloading the start address and SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E) carrying each beat, the bridge advancing the series address by the beat size after every write
* Per-beat read data integrity against the data words written through the bridge
* OKAY terminal status in the SERIES_CTRL readback across the full series with no stuck-Busy condition
* Single-op addressing across many addresses is covered by <<sec-211-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_random_ops_test,2.11>> and <<sec-311-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_read_random_ops_test,3.11>>; every SMC_OTP_AXI_SINGLE_OP (IR=0x1C) shift carries its own address, so reissuing it over a contiguous block is not a distinct bridge property

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a beat-aligned base address, a stride equal to the 32-bit beat, and a beat count N; draw a distinct data word per beat.
. Write the series through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the base address; Update-DR.
b. For each beat i in 0..N-1: shift the i-th data word through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR (IR=0x1E); Update-DR issues the write at base{plus}i*stride and advances the series address.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake.
. Read the series back in the same incrementing order.
a. For each beat i: load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and address base{plus}i*stride; Update-DR.
b. Shift SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI-Lite read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Compare every returned beat with the data word written to that address.
. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY).

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_series_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) after the write and read series
|`smc_otp_series_incr_rdata_chk` |Each returned beat matches the data word written to its address
|`smc_otp_series_incr_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate content over the block matches the issued write series
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every incrementing-address write and read completes, the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY, and all per-beat comparisons match the written data, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-39-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test]]
===== 3.9 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_no_incr_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP fixed-address series write-read

*Test Coverage*:

* Repeated AXI4-Lite writes and reads through the SMC OTP bridge targeting one fixed 32-bit address (no address advance)
* SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) holding one address while SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x1F) carries every beat without advancing it
* Returned data stability when the last-written value is read multiple times
* Series-length robustness with no DR/state corruption between repeated beats, and an unchanged address in the SERIES_CTRL readback

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Choose a single beat-aligned target address and draw N distinct data words.
. Write the N words to the fixed address through the SMC OTP AXI4-Lite bridge.
a. Load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL (IR=0x1D) with op=0b10 (Write), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. For each word: shift it through SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR (IR=0x1F); Update-DR issues the write and leaves the series address unchanged.
c. After each Update-DR, wait for the AXI-Lite write handshake.
. Read the fixed address N times through the bridge.
a. For each iteration: load SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL with op=0b01 (Read), size, and the target address; Update-DR.
b. Shift SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_NO_INCR once to launch the read and wait for the AXI-Lite read handshake; shift it again and take the Capture-DR payload as the returned beat.
. Verify every returned beat equals the last word written.
. Capture SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_CTRL and verify status = 0b00 (OKAY) and address = the target address.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`smc_otp_series_no_incr_status_chk` |SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY (0b00) at the unchanged address after the series
|`smc_otp_series_no_incr_rdata_chk` |Every returned beat equals the last word written
|`smc_otp_series_no_incr_mem_chk` |AXI-Lite subordinate at the fixed address holds the last word written across the series
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All fixed-address beats complete, every returned beat equals the last word written, and the SERIES_CTRL readback reports OKAY at the unchanged address, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-310-dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test]]
===== 3.10 `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_series_write_read_incr_with_error_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_smc_otp_axi_rd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: SMC OTP write-read with error status

*Test Coverage*:

* AXI4-Lite write then read series through the SMC OTP bridge carried by SMC_OTP_AXI_SERIES_DATA_WITH_ERROR_STATUS (IR=0x20), the series-data form whose shift selects the address increment per beat and returns the previous beat's error status
* The increment pattern 1, 0, 1, 1 on both legs: the second beat re-writes the held address, so the series spans three addresses and the later word wins at the held one
