*Pass Criteria*: The unmapped write routes to the CTN xbar default DECERR slave and returns DECERR, no mapped register is disturbed, the XTRIG monitor witnesses the decode miss, and a follow-on legal write succeeds with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-434-dtp_jtag2axi_decode_error_decerr_read_test]]
===== 4.34 `dtp_jtag2axi_decode_error_decerr_read_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_decode_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local XTRIG monitor

*Objective*: CTN default-slave read DECERR

*Test Coverage*:

* CTN AXI-Lite xbar default-slave read DECERR path triggered by a read from an unmapped address
* Xbar `+default_ar_mst_port[_en]+` read-decode branch and default-slave defined read-data response
* No mapped CTN register receives a read-induced side effect (e.g., clear-on-read fields untouched)
* Bridge recovers and accepts a subsequent legal mapped read

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Issue an unmapped read:
a. Drive a JTAG2AXI read from an address with no valid CTN xbar slave so it routes to the default DECERR slave.
. Verify the error response and read data:
a. Confirm the read completion reports DECERR with the defined default read-data value.
b. Confirm the OCAH-local XTRIG monitor observes the read decode miss / default-slave routing event.
. Verify no mapped read-side side effects against the reference model.
. Issue a follow-on legal mapped read and confirm DECERR is not reported and the returned data matches the reference model.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`j2a_decerr_rd_status_chk` |Unmapped read reports DECERR via the default-slave path
|`j2a_decerr_rd_rdata_chk` |Returned read data matches the defined default-slave value
|`j2a_decerr_rd_recovery_chk` |A subsequent legal mapped read returns correct data without DECERR
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The unmapped read routes to the CTN xbar default DECERR slave and returns DECERR with defined read data, no mapped register side effect occurs, the XTRIG monitor witnesses the decode miss, and a follow-on legal read succeeds with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-435-dtp_jtag2axi_decode_error_mixed_test]]
===== 4.35 `dtp_jtag2axi_decode_error_mixed_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_decode_err_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local XTRIG monitor

*Objective*: Mixed mapped/unmapped accesses

*Test Coverage*:

* Alternating mapped and unmapped CTN xbar accesses for stable-path and default-path coverage in the same run
* Both mapped (OKAY) and default-slave (DECERR) routing decisions exercised back to back
* Ordering and per-access status preserved across the mapped/unmapped interleave
* No cross-contamination: an unmapped access neither disturbs mapped state nor corrupts the status of an adjacent mapped access

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Build an interleaved access stream through the JTAG2AXI path:
a. Alternate mapped accesses (valid CTN slave) with unmapped accesses (default DECERR slave), covering both reads and writes.
. Drive the stream and capture per-access outcomes:
a. Confirm each mapped access completes with OKAY and the expected data/side effect.
b. Confirm each unmapped access completes with DECERR via the default slave.
c. Confirm the OCAH-local XTRIG monitor logs each decode decision in issue order.
. Verify ordering and isolation:
a. Confirm status and ordering are preserved across the interleave and that mapped register state is unaffected by the interleaved unmapped accesses.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`j2a_mixed_status_chk` |Each mapped access reports OKAY and each unmapped access reports DECERR
|`j2a_mixed_order_chk` |Per-access status and ordering preserved across the mapped/unmapped interleave
|`j2a_mixed_isolation_chk` |Mapped register state and data unaffected by interleaved unmapped accesses
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The mapped/unmapped interleave exercises both the stable mapped path and the default DECERR path with correct per-access status, ordering is preserved, mapped state is uncontaminated, and the XTRIG monitor witnesses every decode decision with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-436-dtp_jtag2axi_series_corner_all_bridges_test]]
===== 4.36 `dtp_jtag2axi_series_corner_all_bridges_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_series_corner_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: Series corner cases across all bridges

*Test Coverage*:

* Series-operation FIFO depth corners (minimum, maximum, and full/near-full) across all three JTAG2AXI bridges
* Sticky status accumulation and clear behavior over multi-beat series operations
* Address bookkeeping for fixed and incrementing series, including aligned and unaligned start addresses
* Per-bridge independence: each enabled bridge tracks its own series state without aliasing the others
* Read pipelining via `SERIES_CTRL.pl_depth`: with `pl_depth=N`, up to `N{plus}1` reads may be outstanding; the first `N{plus}1` reads return the `DEADBEEF` marker and subsequent reads return real data in deterministic queued order; `pl_depth` swept up to each bridge's reported `+(rd/wr)_pl_depth+` cap

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For each of the three enabled JTAG2AXI bridges, drive a sweep of series operations through `OcahJtagMasterDriver`:
a. Minimum-length and maximum-length series to exercise the series FIFO depth corners.
b. Aligned and unaligned start addresses to exercise address bookkeeping.
c. Fixed and incrementing series to exercise both address-progression modes.
d. Read series with `SERIES_CTRL.pl_depth` swept from 0 to the bridge's reported pipeline-depth cap, issuing `pl_depth{plus}1` outstanding reads.
. Capture per-operation results from the `ocah_axi_vip` subordinate:
a. Confirm the series FIFO accepts each beat up to its depth and reports the expected status at the FIFO corners.
b. Confirm sticky status accumulates correctly across the series and clears as defined.
c. Confirm the final address matches the expected fixed/incrementing progression for each series length.
d. For the pipelined reads, confirm the first `pl_depth{plus}1` reads return the `DEADBEEF` marker and subsequent reads return the subordinate data in deterministic queued order.
. Verify per-bridge isolation: repeat across all three bridges and confirm no bridge's series state, status, or address bookkeeping aliases another.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`j2a_series_fifo_chk` |Series FIFO behaves correctly at minimum/maximum/full depth corners on each bridge
|`j2a_series_status_chk` |Sticky status accumulates and clears correctly across each series operation
|`j2a_series_addr_chk` |Final address matches the expected fixed/incrementing progression for aligned and unaligned starts
|`j2a_series_pldepth_chk` |With `pl_depth=N`, the first `N{plus}1` reads return `DEADBEEF` and later reads return real data in queued order
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All series corner cases (FIFO depth, sticky status, address bookkeeping, and `pl_depth` read pipelining with DEADBEEF marker behavior) complete as expected on each of the three bridges, with no cross-bridge aliasing and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-437-dtp_jtag2axi_dbg_disable_matrix_test]]
===== 4.37 `dtp_jtag2axi_dbg_disable_matrix_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_jtag2axi_dbg_disable_matrix_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, `ocah_axi_vip`

*Objective*: Debug-disable matrix over the three JTAG2AXI bridge gate fields

*Test Coverage*:

* Deterministic one-hot rows for `smc_jtag2axi`, `smc_otp_jtag2axi`, and `sep_otp_jtag2axi`, plus the all-clear and all-disabled boundary masks and seeded multi-hot masks
* Per row and target: an allowed bridge completes a write+readback with real request activity; a blocked bridge shows zero request activity and an intact RAM sentinel, both while gated and after the disable is released (no delayed replay), then recovers with a sanctioned operation
* Mixed rows prove unrelated-target isolation (an enabled bridge keeps working while another is gated)
* Every checker-verified outcome samples its `dbg_disable_cg` cell; the test requires all six bridge cells and emits the JSON coverage artifact

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For each row mask (one-hot per field, all-clear, all-disabled, seeded multi-hot): drive the full disable vector and settle four toggling TCK cycles.
. For each bridge target under the row mask: if enabled, complete a write+readback with activity-counter evidence; if gated, preload a RAM sentinel, shift a write request, and verify zero request activity and the sentinel intact.
. After each row with gated targets: clear all disables without reset, verify every sentinel is still intact (no delayed replay), then complete a sanctioned recovery write+readback per previously-gated target.
. Emit the `dbg_disable_cg` JSON artifact and require all six bridge cells.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`j2a_matrix_allow_chk` |An enabled bridge completes write+readback with real request activity
|`j2a_matrix_block_chk` |A gated bridge produces zero request activity and leaves the RAM sentinel intact
|`j2a_matrix_noreplay_chk` |Sentinels stay intact after the disables release (no delayed replay)
|`j2a_matrix_recovery_chk` |A sanctioned operation succeeds on every previously-gated bridge
|`j2a_matrix_isolation_chk` |Enabled bridges keep working while others are gated
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every row mask produces the per-field allowed/blocked outcomes with no delayed replay and full recovery, closing all six bridge `dbg_disable_cg` cells, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-5-stap-3dcr-and-ijtag-scenarios]]
==== 5. STAP, 3DCR, and iJTAG Scenarios

[[sec-51-dtp_ijtag_sib_all_off_test]]
===== 5.1 `dtp_ijtag_sib_all_off_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ijtag_sib_all_off_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Objective*: All SIBs OFF

*Test Coverage*:

* SELECT_IJTAG (IR=0x1A) with every SIB control bit driven to 0 yields the minimum-length scan chain
* DR chain length equals the SIB-control bits only (3 bits for sec_dft, dft, dfd) with no instrument registers inserted
* None of `jtag_dfd_host_scan_ctrl_o`, `jtag_dft_host_scan_ctrl_o`, `jtag_dft_secure_host_scan_ctrl_o` assert during Shift-DR
* Closed SIBs bypass their downstream instruments so no instrument capture/update occurs

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SELECT_IJTAG (IR=0x1A) into the PTAP via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`.
. Shift a SIB pattern with all three SIB bits = 0 and pulse Update-DR.
. Measure the active DR chain length.
a. Shift a known marker pattern and count the bit delay through the chain.
b. Verify the length equals the 3 SIB-control bits only (no instrument register widths added).
. During Shift-DR, sample all three scan-control outputs and verify none asserts (instruments are bypassed).
. Compare captured/update data against the OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model for the all-off configuration.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sib_all_off_len_chk` |DR chain length equals SIB-control bits only with all instruments bypassed
|`sib_all_off_ctrl_chk` |No DFD/DFT/secure-DFT scan-control output asserts during Shift-DR
|`sib_all_off_data_chk` |Captured/update data matches the SIB reference model for the all-off path
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Chain length collapses to the SIB-control bits, all instrument scan controls stay inactive, and observed data matches the SIB reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-52-dtp_ijtag_sib_all_on_test]]
===== 5.2 `dtp_ijtag_sib_all_on_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ijtag_sib_all_on_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Objective*: All SIBs ON

*Test Coverage*:

* SELECT_IJTAG (IR=0x1A) with all SIB bits = 1 produces the full-length iJTAG scan chain
* DR chain length equals sum of SIB bits plus all three instrument register widths (3 SIBs {plus} 3 instruments)
* All three scan controls (`jtag_dfd_host_scan_ctrl_o`, `jtag_dft_host_scan_ctrl_o`, `jtag_dft_secure_host_scan_ctrl_o`) assert during Shift-DR
* Asserting all three SIB disables (`dbg_disable_i.dft_secure`, `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure`, `dbg_disable_i.dfd`) forces the SIBs closed even when the scan pattern requests all-ON, collapsing the chain
* Access is restored when the disables clear

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SELECT_IJTAG (IR=0x1A) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`.
. Shift a pattern enabling all SIBs (all bits = 1); Update-DR.
. Measure DR chain length by shifting a known marker and counting the delay.
a. Verify length equals the sum of SIB bits plus all three instrument register widths.
. Verify all three host scan-control outputs assert during Shift-DR and instrument data is captured correctly per the SIB model.
. Security gating:
a. Assert `dbg_disable_i.{dft_secure,dft_nonsecure,dfd} = 1`.
b. Re-shift the all-ON pattern; Update-DR.
c. Verify chain length collapses to SIB bits only and no instrument scan control pulses across the whole gated window.
. Clear the disables; re-shift all-ON and confirm full chain and all scan controls restored.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sib_all_on_len_chk` |Full chain length matches sum of SIB bits plus all three instrument widths
|`sib_all_on_ctrl_chk` |All three host scan-control outputs assert during Shift-DR
|`sib_all_on_gate_chk` |Asserted SIB disables force every SIB closed; chain collapses despite all-ON request
|`sib_all_on_restore_chk` |Full chain and scan controls restored after the disables clear
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The all-ON chain reaches full length with every scan control active, collapses to SIB bits when the SIB disables are asserted, and recovers when they clear, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-53-dtp_ijtag_sib_random_test]]
===== 5.3 `dtp_ijtag_sib_random_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ijtag_sib_random_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Objective*: Exhaustive/random SIB patterns

*Test Coverage*:

* All 8 SIB on/off patterns (sec_dft x dft x dfd) walked exhaustively to close `ijtag_sib_pattern` coverage
* Predicted chain length matches sum of (SIB bit {plus} instrument width when SIB ON and ungated, else SIB bit only)
* Only enabled, ungated instruments receive their host scan-control assertions
* Randomized per-SIB disables (`dft_secure`, `dft_nonsecure`, `dfd`) force gated SIBs closed regardless of scan pattern
* Capture/update data tracks the SIB reference model across mixed configurations

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SELECT_IJTAG (IR=0x1A) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`.
. Walk all 8 SIB patterns exhaustively:
a. Shift each (sec_dft, dft, dfd) pattern; Update-DR.
b. Measure chain length and verify it equals SIB bits plus the widths of enabled instruments.
c. Verify scan-control outputs assert only for the enabled instruments.
. For N additional randomized iterations (e.g., 20):
a. Drive a random per-SIB disable mask and a random SIB pattern; Update-DR.
b. Verify gated SIBs (those with their `dbg_disable_i` field asserted) stay closed despite the requested pattern.
c. Verify chain length accounts for forced-closed SIBs and only non-gated enabled instruments receive scan control.
. Compare all captured/update data against the OCAH-local SIB model for each configuration.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`sib_random_len_chk` |Chain length matches predicted SIB-bit {plus} ungated-instrument-width sum
|`sib_random_ctrl_chk` |Scan control asserts only for enabled, ungated instruments
|`sib_random_gate_chk` |Security-gated SIBs forced closed under random disable masks
|`sib_random_data_chk` |Captured/update data matches SIB reference model across all patterns
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All 8 SIB patterns plus randomized security combinations yield correct chain lengths, scan-control assertions, and gating behavior matching the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-54-dtp_ijtag_dft_test]]
===== 5.4 `dtp_ijtag_dft_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ijtag_dft_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Objective*: DFT secure/non-secure access

*Test Coverage*:

* Independent access to the secure and non-secure DFT instruments via their SIBs (SELECT_IJTAG, IR=0x1A)
* Both DFT SIBs may be open simultaneously (no hardware mutual exclusion); chain length sums both instruments
* `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure = 1` gates the non-secure DFT SIB; `dbg_disable_i.dft_secure = 1` gates the secure DFT SIB
* Gating one DFT SIB leaves the other accessible (cross-resource isolation), a gated Update-DR does not modify stored SIB state, and access resumes without TAP reset once the disable clears

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SELECT_IJTAG (IR=0x1A); enable the non-secure DFT SIB; Update-DR.
a. Verify `jtag_dft_host_scan_ctrl_o` asserts and shift a test pattern through the non-secure chain.
. Disable non-secure, enable secure DFT SIB; Update-DR.
a. Verify `jtag_dft_secure_host_scan_ctrl_o` asserts and shift a test pattern through the secure chain.
. Enable both DFT SIBs; Update-DR; verify both scan controls assert and chain length equals the sum of both instruments.
. Secure gating: assert `dbg_disable_i.dft_secure=1`; verify the secure DFT SIB forced closed (its scan controls never pulse across the gated window) while the non-secure SIB remains openable; clear.
. Non-secure gating: assert `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure=1`; verify the non-secure DFT SIB forced closed while the secure SIB remains openable; clear.
. Stored-state evidence: attempt to open a gated SIB, release the disable without reset, verify the gated open attempt did not stick, then confirm a sanctioned open succeeds.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`dft_access_chk` |Secure and non-secure DFT chains each accessible via their SIB with correct scan controls
|`dft_parallel_chk` |Both DFT SIBs open simultaneously; chain length sums both instruments
|`dft_nonsecure_gate_chk` |Non-secure DFT SIB forced closed under `dbg_disable_i.dft_nonsecure`
|`dft_secure_gate_chk` |Secure DFT SIB forced closed under `dbg_disable_i.dft_secure`
|`dft_isolation_chk` |Gating one DFT SIB leaves the other accessible
|`dft_gate_restore_chk` |Both DFT SIBs restored, without TAP reset, after their disables clear
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Secure and non-secure DFT instruments are independently reachable in the same scan flow, each gated by exactly its own disable field and restored when it clears, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-55-dtp_ijtag_dfd_test]]
===== 5.5 `dtp_ijtag_dfd_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ijtag_dfd_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB model

*Objective*: DFD access

*Test Coverage*:

* DFD instrument reachable through the iJTAG network when its SIB is open (SELECT_IJTAG, IR=0x1A)
* `jtag_dfd_host_scan_ctrl_o` asserts shift control and data shifts correctly through `jtag_dfd_host_scan_out_o` / `jtag_dfd_host_scan_in_i`
* `dbg_disable_i.dfd = 1` forces the DFD SIB closed (gated), restored when the disable clears

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Load SELECT_IJTAG (IR=0x1A) via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`; open only the DFD SIB; Update-DR.
. Verify `jtag_dfd_host_scan_ctrl_o` asserts; shift a known pattern out on `jtag_dfd_host_scan_out_o` and read it back through `jtag_dfd_host_scan_in_i` via the OCAH-local SIB model.
. Assert `dbg_disable_i.dfd = 1`; attempt to open the DFD SIB.
a. Verify the SIB stays closed and none of the DFD scan controls pulse across the whole gated window.
. Clear the disable without reset; verify the gated open attempt did not stick, then re-open the DFD SIB and verify DFD access is restored.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`dfd_access_chk` |DFD scan data shifts correctly when the SIB is open and ungated
|`dfd_gate_chk` |DFD SIB forced closed and its scan controls never pulse while `dbg_disable_i.dfd` is asserted
|`dfd_stored_chk` |A gated Update-DR does not modify stored SIB state (no delayed open after release)
|`dfd_restore_chk` |DFD access resumes, without TAP reset, after the disable clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: DFD is reachable when ungated and forced closed when `dbg_disable_i.dfd` is asserted, recovering without reset when the disable clears, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-56-dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_ds_test]]
===== 5.6 `dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_ds_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_ds_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (primary TAP master and the downstream slave TAPs behind every STAP host port), OCAH-local STAP chain model

*Objective*: Downstream (I/O) STAP selection proved end to end against a downstream TAP

*Test Coverage*:

* TAP_3DCR (IR=0x0E) selects the I/O STAP by writing 3DCR `stap_sel=1`, `tms_hold=1`, `config_hold=1` through composed full-chain scans (the `TAP_3DCR` data register is the 2-bit PTAP 3DCR followed serially by the STAP SIB chain); a selected I/O STAP additionally splices one extra flop into the chain through its TDI lockup latch; every scan issued while a STAP is selected is composed over the full network, IR scans included, because the PTAP routes its instruction shift-out into the STAP chain
* A behavioral downstream TAP (`ocah_jtag_vip` slave device: 5-bit IR, distinct IDCODE, one writable `DS_TDR` of a distinct width) is spliced behind every STAP host port; its IR and selected data register become chain segments the reference model predicts independently of the device engine
* While selected, the downstream IDCODE reads back through the STAP, a network-wide IR scan selects `DS_TDR`, and a per-pass random value written through the chain reads back and is confirmed by the device's Update-DR record; the I/O host port forwards (`tdo_oen` pulses, `tms` follows the live TMS) as corroboration
* `dbg_disable_i.stap_io = 1` stops forwarding, parks the host `tms` at the stored `tms_hold=1` so the downstream TAP sits in Test-Logic-Reset, blocks a 3DCR update attempted while gated, and leaves the downstream register unchanged with no Update-DR recorded
* Once the disable clears, without any reset, the stored selection resumes: the downstream answers IDCODE again (park reset proven) and reloading `DS_TDR` returns the pre-gate value; an unrelated STAP's downstream TAP completes a full load/write/read cycle while the target stays gated and records no update; a fresh configuration recovers fully

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Flush the TAP_3DCR configuration chain with one over-length all-zero scan, then configure the I/O STAP with composed scans: open its SIB, then write its 3DCR `stap_sel=1, tms_hold=1, config_hold=1` (PTAP 3DCR held at `select=1, config_hold=1`).
. Run a state-maintaining chain scan inside a temporal window; verify the host `tdo_oen` pulses, host `tms` follows the live TMS, and the captured chain matches the reference model.
. Read the downstream IDCODE through the chain (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`); select `DS_TDR` with a composed IR scan; write a per-pass random value; verify the device recorded exactly one Update-DR with that value (`CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`); read it back through the chain (`CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`).
. Assert `dbg_disable_i.stap_io = 1` and attempt a randomized deselecting 3DCR write:
a. Verify `tdo_oen` never pulses and `tms` parks at the stored `tms_hold=1` across the whole gated window.
b. Verify the chain readback shows the update was ignored (the STAP captures its masked `stap_sel` as 0 while the stored payload survives).
c. Verify the downstream device recorded no Update-DR, still holds the written value (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`), and sits in Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`).
. Clear the disable without reset; verify forwarding resumes from the stored selection, the downstream answers IDCODE again, and reloading `DS_TDR` reads back the pre-gate value (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`).
. Re-assert the disable and configure a neighbor STAP; verify the neighbor forwards while the gated port stays quiet, the neighbor's downstream TAP completes a load/write/read cycle, and the gated port's device records no update (isolation).
. Clear all disables, flush (composed IR load parking the spliced downstream TAPs on BYPASS), and configure fresh; verify full recovery with a new `DS_TDR` write and readback.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`stap_ds_select_chk` |Selected I/O STAP forwards: `tdo_oen` pulses and `tms` follows the live TMS (`CHK-SCAN-WIN`)
|`stap_ds_readback_chk` |Composed chain readback matches the reference model in every state (`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`)
|`stap_ds_downstream_chk` |Downstream IDCODE and `DS_TDR` read back through the selected STAP; the device's Update-DR record matches the write (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`, `CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`)
|`stap_ds_gate_chk` |With `dbg_disable_i.stap_io` asserted, forwarding stops, a gated 3DCR update is ignored, and the downstream register is frozen in Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`, `CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`)
|`stap_ds_isolation_chk` |An unrelated STAP and its downstream TAP stay usable while the I/O STAP is gated
|`stap_ds_recover_chk` |Selection resumes without reset after release with the pre-gate downstream value intact (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`), and a fresh configuration fully recovers
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The I/O STAP forwards only while selected and enabled, the downstream TAP behind it is programmable and readable exactly then, `dbg_disable_i.stap_io` alone gates it with downstream state preserved and no delayed effect, and recovery needs no reset, with every required `CHK-*` ID recorded and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-57-dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_smc_test]]
===== 5.7 `dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_smc_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_smc_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (primary TAP master and the downstream slave TAPs behind every STAP host port), OCAH-local STAP chain model

*Objective*: SMC STAP selection proved end to end against a downstream TAP

*Test Coverage*:

* TAP_3DCR (IR=0x0E) selects the SMC debug STAP by writing 3DCR `stap_sel=1`, `tms_hold=1`, `config_hold=1` through composed full-chain scans (the `TAP_3DCR` data register is the 2-bit PTAP 3DCR followed serially by the STAP SIB chain); every scan issued while a STAP is selected is composed over the full network, IR scans included, because the PTAP routes its instruction shift-out into the STAP chain
* A behavioral downstream TAP (`ocah_jtag_vip` slave device: 5-bit IR, distinct IDCODE, one writable `DS_TDR` of a distinct width) is spliced behind every STAP host port; its IR and selected data register become chain segments the reference model predicts independently of the device engine
* While selected, the downstream IDCODE reads back through the STAP, a network-wide IR scan selects `DS_TDR`, and a per-pass random value written through the chain reads back and is confirmed by the device's Update-DR record; the SMC host port forwards (`tdo_oen` pulses, `tms` follows the live TMS) as corroboration
* `dbg_disable_i.stap_smc = 1` stops forwarding, parks the host `tms` at the stored `tms_hold=1` so the downstream TAP sits in Test-Logic-Reset, blocks a 3DCR update attempted while gated, and leaves the downstream register unchanged with no Update-DR recorded
* Once the disable clears, without any reset, the stored selection resumes: the downstream answers IDCODE again (park reset proven) and reloading `DS_TDR` returns the pre-gate value; an unrelated STAP's downstream TAP completes a full load/write/read cycle while the target stays gated and records no update; a fresh configuration recovers fully

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Flush the TAP_3DCR configuration chain with one over-length all-zero scan, then configure the SMC debug STAP with composed scans: open its SIB, then write its 3DCR `stap_sel=1, tms_hold=1, config_hold=1` (PTAP 3DCR held at `select=1, config_hold=1`).
. Run a state-maintaining chain scan inside a temporal window; verify the host `tdo_oen` pulses, host `tms` follows the live TMS, and the captured chain matches the reference model.
. Read the downstream IDCODE through the chain (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`); select `DS_TDR` with a composed IR scan; write a per-pass random value; verify the device recorded exactly one Update-DR with that value (`CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`); read it back through the chain (`CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`).
. Assert `dbg_disable_i.stap_smc = 1` and attempt a randomized deselecting 3DCR write:
a. Verify `tdo_oen` never pulses and `tms` parks at the stored `tms_hold=1` across the whole gated window.
b. Verify the chain readback shows the update was ignored (the STAP captures its masked `stap_sel` as 0 while the stored payload survives).
c. Verify the downstream device recorded no Update-DR, still holds the written value (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`), and sits in Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`).
. Clear the disable without reset; verify forwarding resumes from the stored selection, the downstream answers IDCODE again, and reloading `DS_TDR` reads back the pre-gate value (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`).
. Re-assert the disable and configure a neighbor STAP; verify the neighbor forwards while the gated port stays quiet, the neighbor's downstream TAP completes a load/write/read cycle, and the gated port's device records no update (isolation).
. Clear all disables, flush (composed IR load parking the spliced downstream TAPs on BYPASS), and configure fresh; verify full recovery with a new `DS_TDR` write and readback.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`stap_smc_select_chk` |Selected SMC debug STAP forwards: `tdo_oen` pulses and `tms` follows the live TMS (`CHK-SCAN-WIN`)
|`stap_smc_readback_chk` |Composed chain readback matches the reference model in every state (`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`)
|`stap_smc_downstream_chk` |Downstream IDCODE and `DS_TDR` read back through the selected STAP; the device's Update-DR record matches the write (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`, `CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`)
|`stap_smc_gate_chk` |With `dbg_disable_i.stap_smc` asserted, forwarding stops, a gated 3DCR update is ignored, and the downstream register is frozen in Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`, `CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`)
|`stap_smc_isolation_chk` |An unrelated STAP and its downstream TAP stay usable while the SMC debug STAP is gated
|`stap_smc_recover_chk` |Selection resumes without reset after release with the pre-gate downstream value intact (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`), and a fresh configuration fully recovers
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The SMC debug STAP forwards only while selected and enabled, the downstream TAP behind it is programmable and readable exactly then, `dbg_disable_i.stap_smc` alone gates it with downstream state preserved and no delayed effect, and recovery needs no reset, with every required `CHK-*` ID recorded and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-58-dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_extra_test]]
===== 5.8 `dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_extra_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_extra_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (primary TAP master and the downstream slave TAPs behind every STAP host port), OCAH-local STAP chain model

*Objective*: Extra STAP selection proved end to end against a downstream TAP

*Test Coverage*:

* TAP_3DCR (IR=0x0E) selects the extra STAP by writing 3DCR `stap_sel=1`, `tms_hold=1`, `config_hold=1` through composed full-chain scans (the `TAP_3DCR` data register is the 2-bit PTAP 3DCR followed serially by the STAP SIB chain); every scan issued while a STAP is selected is composed over the full network, IR scans included, because the PTAP routes its instruction shift-out into the STAP chain
* A behavioral downstream TAP (`ocah_jtag_vip` slave device: 5-bit IR, distinct IDCODE, one writable `DS_TDR` of a distinct width) is spliced behind every STAP host port; its IR and selected data register become chain segments the reference model predicts independently of the device engine
* While selected, the downstream IDCODE reads back through the STAP, a network-wide IR scan selects `DS_TDR`, and a per-pass random value written through the chain reads back and is confirmed by the device's Update-DR record; the `jtag_stap_extra_host_tap_ctrl_o[0]` port forwards (`tdo_oen` pulses, `tms` follows the live TMS) as corroboration
* `dbg_disable_i.stap_extra = 1` stops forwarding, parks the host `tms` at the stored `tms_hold=1` so the downstream TAP sits in Test-Logic-Reset, blocks a 3DCR update attempted while gated, and leaves the downstream register unchanged with no Update-DR recorded
* Once the disable clears, without any reset, the stored selection resumes: the downstream answers IDCODE again (park reset proven) and reloading `DS_TDR` returns the pre-gate value; an unrelated STAP's downstream TAP completes a full load/write/read cycle while the target stays gated and records no update; a fresh configuration recovers fully

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Flush the TAP_3DCR configuration chain with one over-length all-zero scan, then configure the extra STAP with composed scans: open its SIB, then write its 3DCR `stap_sel=1, tms_hold=1, config_hold=1` (PTAP 3DCR held at `select=1, config_hold=1`).
. Run a state-maintaining chain scan inside a temporal window; verify the host `tdo_oen` pulses, host `tms` follows the live TMS, and the captured chain matches the reference model.
. Read the downstream IDCODE through the chain (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`); select `DS_TDR` with a composed IR scan; write a per-pass random value; verify the device recorded exactly one Update-DR with that value (`CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`); read it back through the chain (`CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`).
. Assert `dbg_disable_i.stap_extra = 1` and attempt a randomized deselecting 3DCR write:
a. Verify `tdo_oen` never pulses and `tms` parks at the stored `tms_hold=1` across the whole gated window.
b. Verify the chain readback shows the update was ignored (the STAP captures its masked `stap_sel` as 0 while the stored payload survives).
c. Verify the downstream device recorded no Update-DR, still holds the written value (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`), and sits in Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`).
. Clear the disable without reset; verify forwarding resumes from the stored selection, the downstream answers IDCODE again, and reloading `DS_TDR` reads back the pre-gate value (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`).
. Re-assert the disable and configure a neighbor STAP; verify the neighbor forwards while the gated port stays quiet, the neighbor's downstream TAP completes a load/write/read cycle, and the gated port's device records no update (isolation).
. Clear all disables, flush (composed IR load parking the spliced downstream TAPs on BYPASS), and configure fresh; verify full recovery with a new `DS_TDR` write and readback.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`stap_extra_select_chk` |Selected extra STAP forwards: `tdo_oen` pulses and `tms` follows the live TMS (`CHK-SCAN-WIN`)
|`stap_extra_readback_chk` |Composed chain readback matches the reference model in every state (`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`)
|`stap_extra_downstream_chk` |Downstream IDCODE and `DS_TDR` read back through the selected STAP; the device's Update-DR record matches the write (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`, `CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`)
|`stap_extra_gate_chk` |With `dbg_disable_i.stap_extra` asserted, forwarding stops, a gated 3DCR update is ignored, and the downstream register is frozen in Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`, `CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`)
|`stap_extra_isolation_chk` |An unrelated STAP and its downstream TAP stay usable while the extra STAP is gated
|`stap_extra_recover_chk` |Selection resumes without reset after release with the pre-gate downstream value intact (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`), and a fresh configuration fully recovers
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The extra STAP forwards only while selected and enabled, the downstream TAP behind it is programmable and readable exactly then, `dbg_disable_i.stap_extra` alone gates it with downstream state preserved and no delayed effect, and recovery needs no reset, with every required `CHK-*` ID recorded and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-59-dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_sep_test]]
===== 5.9 `dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_sep_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_3dcr_stap_sel_sep_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip` (primary TAP master and the downstream slave TAPs behind every STAP host port), OCAH-local STAP chain model

*Objective*: SEP STAP selection proved end to end against a downstream TAP

*Test Coverage*:

* TAP_3DCR (IR=0x0E) selects the SEP debug STAP by writing 3DCR `stap_sel=1`, `tms_hold=1`, `config_hold=1` through composed full-chain scans (the `TAP_3DCR` data register is the 2-bit PTAP 3DCR followed serially by the STAP SIB chain); every scan issued while a STAP is selected is composed over the full network, IR scans included, because the PTAP routes its instruction shift-out into the STAP chain
* A behavioral downstream TAP (`ocah_jtag_vip` slave device: 5-bit IR, distinct IDCODE, one writable `DS_TDR` of a distinct width) is spliced behind every STAP host port; its IR and selected data register become chain segments the reference model predicts independently of the device engine
* While selected, the downstream IDCODE reads back through the STAP, a network-wide IR scan selects `DS_TDR`, and a per-pass random value written through the chain reads back and is confirmed by the device's Update-DR record; the SEP host port forwards (`tdo_oen` pulses, `tms` follows the live TMS) as corroboration
* `dbg_disable_i.stap_sep = 1` stops forwarding, parks the host `tms` at the stored `tms_hold=1` so the downstream TAP sits in Test-Logic-Reset, blocks a 3DCR update attempted while gated, and leaves the downstream register unchanged with no Update-DR recorded
* Once the disable clears, without any reset, the stored selection resumes: the downstream answers IDCODE again (park reset proven) and reloading `DS_TDR` returns the pre-gate value; an unrelated STAP's downstream TAP completes a full load/write/read cycle while the target stays gated and records no update; a fresh configuration recovers fully

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Flush the TAP_3DCR configuration chain with one over-length all-zero scan, then configure the SEP debug STAP with composed scans: open its SIB, then write its 3DCR `stap_sel=1, tms_hold=1, config_hold=1` (PTAP 3DCR held at `select=1, config_hold=1`).
. Run a state-maintaining chain scan inside a temporal window; verify the host `tdo_oen` pulses, host `tms` follows the live TMS, and the captured chain matches the reference model.
. Read the downstream IDCODE through the chain (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`); select `DS_TDR` with a composed IR scan; write a per-pass random value; verify the device recorded exactly one Update-DR with that value (`CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`); read it back through the chain (`CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`).
. Assert `dbg_disable_i.stap_sep = 1` and attempt a randomized deselecting 3DCR write:
a. Verify `tdo_oen` never pulses and `tms` parks at the stored `tms_hold=1` across the whole gated window.
b. Verify the chain readback shows the update was ignored (the STAP captures its masked `stap_sel` as 0 while the stored payload survives).
c. Verify the downstream device recorded no Update-DR, still holds the written value (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`), and sits in Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`).
. Clear the disable without reset; verify forwarding resumes from the stored selection, the downstream answers IDCODE again, and reloading `DS_TDR` reads back the pre-gate value (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`).
. Re-assert the disable and configure a neighbor STAP; verify the neighbor forwards while the gated port stays quiet, the neighbor's downstream TAP completes a load/write/read cycle, and the gated port's device records no update (isolation).
. Clear all disables, flush (composed IR load parking the spliced downstream TAPs on BYPASS), and configure fresh; verify full recovery with a new `DS_TDR` write and readback.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`stap_sep_select_chk` |Selected SEP debug STAP forwards: `tdo_oen` pulses and `tms` follows the live TMS (`CHK-SCAN-WIN`)
|`stap_sep_readback_chk` |Composed chain readback matches the reference model in every state (`CHK-SCAN-CHAIN`)
|`stap_sep_downstream_chk` |Downstream IDCODE and `DS_TDR` read back through the selected STAP; the device's Update-DR record matches the write (`CHK-DS-IDCODE`, `CHK-DS-TDR-READBACK`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE`, `CHK-SLAVE-DR-UPDATE-COUNT`)
|`stap_sep_gate_chk` |With `dbg_disable_i.stap_sep` asserted, forwarding stops, a gated 3DCR update is ignored, and the downstream register is frozen in Test-Logic-Reset (`CHK-DS-TDR-HOLD`, `CHK-DS-PARKED-TLR`)
|`stap_sep_isolation_chk` |An unrelated STAP and its downstream TAP stay usable while the SEP debug STAP is gated
|`stap_sep_recover_chk` |Selection resumes without reset after release with the pre-gate downstream value intact (`CHK-DS-TDR-RESUME`), and a fresh configuration fully recovers
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The SEP debug STAP forwards only while selected and enabled, the downstream TAP behind it is programmable and readable exactly then, `dbg_disable_i.stap_sep` alone gates it with downstream state preserved and no delayed effect, and recovery needs no reset, with every required `CHK-*` ID recorded and zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-510-dtp_ext_stap_scan_test]]
===== 5.10 `dtp_ext_stap_scan_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ext_stap_scan_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local STAP model

*Objective*: Extended STAP scan

*Test Coverage*:

* Extended STAP scan interface (`+jtag_stap_host_scan_*+`) enabled via PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=1
* `jtag_stap_host_scan_ctrl_o` asserts scan control during DR operations and data routes through `jtag_stap_host_scan_out_o` / `jtag_stap_host_scan_in_i`
* Clearing PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=0 deasserts the scan interface
* `dbg_disable_i.stap_host = 1` forces all four `jtag_stap_host_scan_ctrl_o` signals quiet across the whole gated window (temporal-window evidence) and bypasses scan-in (PTAP receives the `stap_host_scan_out` loopback instead of external scan-in)
* Normal scan operation resumes, without any reset, after the disable clears

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Enable the extended STAP scan interface by writing PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=1 via `OcahJtagMasterDriver`.
. Inside a temporal window, verify `jtag_stap_host_scan_ctrl_o` select and shift-enable pulse during DR operations; shift a known pattern out on `jtag_stap_host_scan_out_o`.
. Verify the data returned on `jtag_stap_host_scan_in_i` matches the expected loopback/known response.
. Clear PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=0 and verify scan control deasserts.
. Security gating:
a. Re-enable scan (PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=1), then assert `dbg_disable_i.stap_host = 1`.
b. Inside a temporal window covering the whole gated DR operation, verify none of the four host scan-control signals pulses and scan-in is disconnected (PTAP sees the `stap_host_scan_out` loopback rather than external scan-in).
c. Clear the disable without reset and verify scan control and the data path are restored.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ext_stap_enable_chk` |Scan control and data signals function when PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=1
|`ext_stap_disable_chk` |Scan interface deactivates when PTAP_3DCR.SELECT=0
|`ext_stap_gate_chk` |With `dbg_disable_i.stap_host` asserted, all four host scan controls stay quiet across the gated window and scan-in is bypassed
|`ext_stap_restore_chk` |Normal STAP host scan resumes, without reset, after the disable clears
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The extended STAP scan interface operates when selected, deactivates when deselected, is suppressed and loopback-bypassed while `dbg_disable_i.stap_host` is asserted, and recovers without reset when it clears, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-511-dtp_3dcr_config_hold_test]]
===== 5.11 `dtp_3dcr_config_hold_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_3dcr_config_hold_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local STAP model

*Objective*: 3DCR config hold

*Test Coverage*:

* CONFIG_HOLD in both PTAP_3DCR and STAP 3DCR protects register contents across Test-Logic-Reset (TLR)
* With CONFIG_HOLD=1, SELECT (PTAP) and SELECT/TMS_HOLD (STAP) survive a forced TLR
* With CONFIG_HOLD=0, a TLR clears the affected 3DCR fields to 0
* Configuration values are exercised through `OcahJtagMasterDriver` IR/DR scans against the STAP reference model

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Write PTAP_3DCR with CONFIG_HOLD=1, SELECT=1; Update-DR.
. Write the STAP 3DCR with CONFIG_HOLD=1, SELECT=1, TMS_HOLD=1; Update-DR.
. Force TLR (5x TMS=1).
a. Read PTAP_3DCR and verify SELECT=1 preserved.
b. Read the STAP 3DCR and verify SELECT=1 and TMS_HOLD=1 preserved.
. Clear CONFIG_HOLD=0 in both PTAP and STAP 3DCRs; force TLR again.
a. Read both 3DCRs and verify all bits cleared to 0.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`config_hold_preserve_chk` |CONFIG_HOLD=1 preserves PTAP SELECT and STAP SELECT/TMS_HOLD across TLR
|`config_hold_clear_chk` |CONFIG_HOLD=0 lets TLR clear the 3DCR fields to 0
|`config_hold_data_chk` |3DCR readback through `OcahJtagMasterDriver` matches the STAP reference model
|===

*Pass Criteria*: CONFIG_HOLD=1 holds both 3DCRs across TLR while CONFIG_HOLD=0 allows TLR to clear them, matching the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-512-dtp_3dcr_tms_hold_test]]
===== 5.12 `dtp_3dcr_tms_hold_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_3dcr_tms_hold_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local STAP model

*Objective*: 3DCR TMS hold

*Test Coverage*:

* TMS_HOLD field in the STAP 3DCR sets the parked TMS value driven to a deselected STAP
* TMS_HOLD=1 parks the deselected STAP TMS high (drives toward TLR); TMS_HOLD=0 parks it low (drives toward RTI)
* Parked value is observed on the STAP TAP control output while the STAP is deselected (SELECT=0)
* Both TMS_HOLD polarities are exercised through `OcahJtagMasterDriver` 3DCR scans

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Select a STAP via the 3DCR (SELECT=1, TMS_HOLD=1); Update-DR.
. Deselect the STAP (SELECT=0, TMS_HOLD=1); Update-DR.
a. Verify the STAP TAP control output drives TMS=1 (parked high toward TLR).
. Repeat with TMS_HOLD=0:
a. Select then deselect the STAP with TMS_HOLD=0; Update-DR.
b. Verify the STAP TAP control output drives TMS=0 (parked low toward RTI).
. Confirm the parked TMS tracks the configured TMS_HOLD against the STAP reference model.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`tms_hold_high_chk` |Deselected STAP TMS parks high (TLR) when TMS_HOLD=1
|`tms_hold_low_chk` |Deselected STAP TMS parks low (RTI) when TMS_HOLD=0
|`tms_hold_data_chk` |Parked TMS value matches the STAP reference model for each polarity
|===

*Pass Criteria*: A deselected STAP parks its TMS at the configured TMS_HOLD value for both polarities, matching the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-513-dtp_scan_dbg_disable_matrix_test]]
===== 5.13 `dtp_scan_dbg_disable_matrix_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_scan_dbg_disable_matrix_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_jtag_vip`, OCAH-local iJTAG SIB and STAP chain models

*Objective*: Debug-disable matrix over the eight scan-side gate fields

*Test Coverage*:

* Deterministic one-hot rows for `stap_io`, `stap_smc`, `stap_sep`, `stap_extra`, `stap_host`, `dft_secure`, `dft_nonsecure`, and `dfd`, plus the all-clear and all-disabled boundary masks and seeded multi-hot masks
* Per row: each iJTAG SIB requested open follows its disable (temporal window: a gated SIB's four scan controls never pulse, an effective SIB's select is seen high); each STAP configured through composed TAP_3DCR scans follows its disable (a gated port never forwards and its gated 3DCR write is ignored, verified by chain readback); the extended host scan interface follows `stap_host`
* After the all-disabled row: releasing every disable without reset shows no gated open attempt stuck (release without replay), and a full all-clear row recovers
* Mixed rows prove unrelated-resource isolation; every checker-verified outcome samples its `dbg_disable_cg` cell; the test requires all sixteen scan-side cells and emits the JSON coverage artifact

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For each row mask (all-clear, one-hot per field, seeded multi-hot, all-disabled last): drive the full disable vector and settle four toggling TCK cycles.
. iJTAG evidence: request all three SIBs open inside a temporal window and check each SIB's allowed/blocked outcome against the reference model.
. STAP evidence: flush the TAP_3DCR chain, open all four SIBs and write all four 3DCR payloads in composed scans under the row mask, then run a windowed maintain scan; check per-port forwarding vs quiet+parked outcomes, the host scan-control window, and the chain readback (gated payload writes ignored).
. After the final all-disabled row: clear all disables, observe without reprogramming, and verify no SIB select pulses (no delayed open); then rerun the all-clear row as recovery.
. Emit the `dbg_disable_cg` JSON artifact and require all sixteen scan-side cells.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`scan_matrix_ijtag_chk` |Each SIB's select/window outcome follows exactly its disable field
|`scan_matrix_stap_chk` |Each STAP's forwarding outcome follows exactly its disable field; gated 3DCR writes are ignored
|`scan_matrix_host_chk` |The extended host scan controls follow `stap_host`
|`scan_matrix_readback_chk` |Composed chain readbacks match the reference model under every mask
|`scan_matrix_noreplay_chk` |No gated open attempt sticks after the disables release
|`scan_matrix_recovery_chk` |The all-clear row fully recovers after the all-disabled row
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every row mask produces the per-field allowed/blocked outcomes with temporal-window evidence, no delayed effects, and full recovery, closing all sixteen scan-side `dbg_disable_cg` cells, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-6-xtrig-ctp-and-ctm-scenarios]]
==== 6. XTRIG, CTP, and CTM Scenarios

[[sec-61-dtp_xtrig_wire_or_test]]
===== 6.1 `dtp_xtrig_wire_or_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_xtrig_wire_or_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTP/CTM BFMs

*Objective*: CTP wire-OR

*Test Coverage*:

* CTP wire-OR mode (CONFIG.MODE=0) pulse synchronization with configurable pulse stretching
* Output pulse width equals (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1) clock cycles for both a wide multiplier and the single-cycle minimum
* External CT_Req_in input synchronized into the `clk_i` domain before delivery
* STATUS.BUSY accurately reflects pulse activity (asserts during the stretched pulse, clears after)
* Internal cross-trigger pulse delivered to the CTM destination on an external trigger

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure CTP[0] via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`: CONFIG.MODE=0 (Wire-OR), CONFIG.INVERT=0, STRETCH_MULT=0x000F (16 clocks).
. Drive a single-cycle internal cross-trigger request to CTP[0] (via CTM routing).
a. Observe CT_Req_out on the CTP BFM; verify it asserts and remains asserted for exactly (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1) clock cycles.
b. Read CTP[0] STATUS; verify BUSY=1 while the stretched pulse is active and BUSY=0 after it completes.
. Drive a short CT_Req_in pulse from the CTP BFM (simulate an external trigger).
a. Verify the input is synchronized to `clk_i` and the resulting internal cross-trigger pulse is delivered to the programmed CTM destination.
. Reprogram STRETCH_MULT=0x0000 (1 clock), repeat the internal trigger, and verify a single-cycle output pulse with the same BUSY behavior.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctp_wire_or_csr_chk` |CONFIG/STRETCH_MULT writes read back the programmed route configuration
|`ctp_wire_or_stretch_chk` |Output pulse width equals (STRETCH_MULT{plus}1) clocks for both multiplier values
|`ctp_wire_or_sync_chk` |External CT_Req_in is synchronized to `clk_i` and delivered to the CTM destination
|`ctp_wire_or_busy_chk` |STATUS.BUSY asserts during the stretched pulse and clears afterward
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Wire-OR pulse stretching, input synchronization, and BUSY tracking match the reference model for both stretch settings, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-62-dtp_xtrig_p2p_test]]
===== 6.2 `dtp_xtrig_p2p_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_xtrig_p2p_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTP/CTM BFMs

*Objective*: CTP point-to-point

*Test Coverage*:

* CTP point-to-point 4-phase handshake (CONFIG.MODE=1) in both directions
* Request path FSM sequences REQ_PHASE1-++>++REQ_PHASE2-++>++IDLE; response path sequences RESP_PHASE1-++>++RESP_PHASE2-++>++IDLE
* STATUS register reflects live handshake state and STATUS.BUSY tracks the in-flight handshake
* Internal cross-trigger delivered to the CTM destination on an external trigger

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure CTP[0] via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`: CONFIG.MODE=1 (Point-to-Point), CONFIG.INVERT=0.
. Drive an internal cross-trigger request to CTP[0] (via CTM routing).
a. Verify CT_Req_out asserts on the CTP BFM.
b. Drive CT_Ack_in from the CTP BFM (simulate the external responder).
c. Verify the handshake completes: CT_Req_out deasserts, CT_Ack_in deasserts, FSM returns to IDLE.
. Read CTP[0] STATUS; verify BUSY=0 after completion.
. Drive CT_Req_in from the CTP BFM (simulate an external trigger).
a. Verify CT_Ack_out asserts and the response-side handshake completes through RESP_PHASE1-++>++RESP_PHASE2-++>++IDLE.
b. Verify the internal cross-trigger is delivered to the programmed CTM destination.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctp_p2p_handshake_chk` |Req/Ack 4-phase handshake completes in both directions
|`ctp_p2p_fsm_chk` |FSM sequences through REQ_PHASE1/2 and RESP_PHASE1/2 back to IDLE
|`ctp_p2p_busy_chk` |STATUS.BUSY asserts during and clears after each handshake
|`ctp_p2p_deliver_chk` |External trigger is delivered to the CTM destination
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Both handshake directions complete with correct FSM sequencing, BUSY behavior, and trigger delivery, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-63-dtp_xtrig_reset_test]]
===== 6.3 `dtp_xtrig_reset_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_xtrig_reset_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTP/CTM BFMs

*Objective*: CTP reset recovery

*Test Coverage*:

* CONFIG.RESET recovery from a P2P handshake deadlock (stalled external ack)
* System reset (`rst_n_i`) clears all CTP register state back to defaults
* No spurious GPIO/trigger activity asserted during either reset path
* Normal operation resumes after recovery (a fresh handshake proceeds)

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure CTP[0] in P2P mode and initiate a handshake.
a. Stall the external CT_Ack_in on the CTP BFM to simulate a deadlock.
b. Read STATUS; verify BUSY=1 (handshake stuck).
. Recover via CONFIG.RESET.
a. Write CONFIG.RESET=1 via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`.
b. Verify the handshake is force-cleared: STATUS.BUSY=0 and CT_Req_out deasserts.
c. Write CONFIG.RESET=0 to release; verify CTP returns to normal operation and a new handshake can proceed to completion.
. Assert system reset (`rst_n_i`).
a. Verify all CTP registers return to reset/default values on readback after release.
b. Verify no spurious GPIO toggles occur during the reset window.
. Reprogram a post-reset route and verify it operates correctly.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctp_config_reset_chk` |CONFIG.RESET clears a stalled P2P handshake (BUSY=0, CT_Req_out deasserts)
|`ctp_sys_reset_chk` |System reset returns all CTP CSRs to default values
|`ctp_reset_no_spurious_chk` |No spurious GPIO/trigger activity during either reset
|`ctp_reset_recovery_chk` |CTP resumes normal operation and a post-reset handshake completes
|===

*Pass Criteria*: CONFIG.RESET recovers from deadlock, system reset restores defaults with no spurious activity, and post-reset operation matches the reference model, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-64-dtp_xtrig_rand_test]]
===== 6.4 `dtp_xtrig_rand_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_xtrig_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTP/CTM BFMs

*Objective*: Random CTP configuration

*Test Coverage*:

* Randomized CTP configuration across multiple CTP indices, both modes, and varied settings
* MODE x INVERT x STRETCH_MULT combination coverage
* INVERT correctly flips GPIO signal polarity in both Wire-OR and P2P modes
* STRETCH_MULT respected in Wire-OR mode for each randomized value

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. For N iterations (e.g., 20):
a. Randomly select a CTP index.
b. Randomly choose a legal configuration: MODE (0/1), INVERT (0/1), STRETCH_MULT (random 16-bit).
c. Write CONFIG and STRETCH_MULT via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`.
d. Drive a cross-trigger event (internal via CTM routing or external via the CTP BFM).
e. Verify behavior matches the reference model for the configured mode and settings.
f. Verify INVERT correctly inverts the GPIO signal polarity.
. Compare the observed trigger routing/handshake stream against the software reference model across all iterations.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctp_rand_csr_chk` |Random CONFIG/STRETCH_MULT writes read back as programmed
|`ctp_rand_mode_chk` |Observed behavior matches the configured Wire-OR or P2P mode
|`ctp_rand_invert_chk` |INVERT flips GPIO polarity in both modes
|`ctp_rand_route_chk` |Observed trigger routing matches the reference model across iterations
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All randomized configurations produce reference-model-matching behavior, including INVERT polarity and STRETCH_MULT timing, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-65-dtp_xtrig_rand_deterministic_csr_sweep_test]]
===== 6.5 `dtp_xtrig_rand_deterministic_csr_sweep_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_xtrig_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTP BFM

*Objective*: CTP CSR deterministic sweep

*Test Coverage*:

* Deterministic walk of every XTRIG/CTP regblock CSR field (coverage-closure of byte-enable and masked-write condition holes)
* Per-byte byte-enable writes that random selection can miss in the CTP generated regblock
* Reserved-bit / legal-mask behavior and aliasing checks across CTP CSRs
* Generated routing state produced by each programmed value

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Bring the XTRIG CSR path to idle after reset and disable all CTP routing.
. Walk every CTP CSR field with deterministic values.
a. Drive full-word and per-byte byte-enable writes through `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent` to exercise the regblock byte-enable / masked-write conditions.
b. Cover single-bit, all-ones, and reserved-bit patterns per field.
. Read back each write and verify the defined field reflects the programmed value and that reserved bits behave as specified.
. Verify no CTP CSR aliases another (each offset is independent) and that the resulting routing state matches the reference model.
. Disable all CTP routing before test exit.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctp_csr_sweep_byteen_chk` |Per-byte byte-enable writes update only the addressed bytes
|`ctp_csr_sweep_readback_chk` |Every programmed CSR field reads back its written value
|`ctp_csr_sweep_reserved_chk` |Reserved bits and legal masks behave as specified
|`ctp_csr_sweep_alias_chk` |No CTP CSR offset aliases another
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every CTP CSR field accepts and returns deterministic values across all byte-enable patterns with correct reserved-bit and routing behavior and no aliasing, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-66-dtp_xtrig_rand_deterministic_dst_port_sweep_test]]
===== 6.6 `dtp_xtrig_rand_deterministic_dst_port_sweep_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_xtrig_rand_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTP/CTM BFMs

*Objective*: Destination-port toggle sweep

*Test Coverage*:

* Deterministic sweep of every CTP destination-port selection (coverage-closure of destination-port toggles random selection can miss)
* Internal CT ack toggles exercised per destination
* Exactly the programmed destination toggles for a single source trigger (one-hot delivery)
* No unintended destination ports toggle

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Disable all CTM/CTP routing and idle the CSR path after reset.
. Sweep each destination port selection deterministically.
a. Program one destination-port mask at a time via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`.
b. Drive a single source trigger via CTM routing.
c. Observe the CTP/CTM BFM outputs and verify exactly the programmed destination toggles while all others remain idle.
d. Exercise the internal CT ack toggle for that destination.
. Repeat across all configured destination-port selections.
. Compare the full toggle sequence against the reference model and disable routing on exit.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctp_dst_port_sweep_csr_chk` |Destination-port masks read back as programmed
|`ctp_dst_port_sweep_onehot_chk` |Exactly the programmed destination toggles per source trigger
|`ctp_dst_port_sweep_ack_chk` |Internal CT ack toggles for the selected destination
|`ctp_dst_port_sweep_iso_chk` |No unprogrammed destination port toggles
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Every destination-port selection toggles exactly its programmed destination with correct ack behavior and no spurious toggles, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-67-dtp_xtrig_reg_stall_test]]
===== 6.7 `dtp_xtrig_reg_stall_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_xtrig_reg_stall_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local XTRIG monitor

*Objective*: XTRIG regblock accepted-access path

*Test Coverage*:

* The upstream AXI-Lite path always presents the XTRIG regblock a ready interface, so the regblock local stall (back-pressure) paths are unreachable in the DTP integration
* Confirms XTRIG CSR writes and reads complete without corruption when the register interface is exercised
* No trigger state changes occur until an access is accepted

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Exercise representative XTRIG CSR writes and reads through `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent` to confirm the reachable accepted-access path.
a. Verify each write/read completes without corruption.
b. Verify no trigger state changes until the access is accepted by the regblock.
. Confirm through the XTRIG monitor trace that the regblock local stall condition is never asserted under the exercised accesses.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`xtrig_reg_stall_unreachable_chk` |The regblock local stall condition is never asserted under the exercised accesses
|`xtrig_reg_stall_csr_chk` |Accepted XTRIG CSR writes/reads read back without corruption
|`xtrig_reg_stall_no_premature_chk` |No trigger state changes until the access is accepted
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Accepted XTRIG CSR accesses complete uncorrupted and the regblock local stall condition is never observed, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-68-dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_test]]
===== 6.8 `dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local XTRIG monitor

*Objective*: Manual AXI-Lite channel skew

*Test Coverage*:

* Manual XTRIG AXI-Lite AW/W/R/B channel skew for ready-low and response-hold coverage
* Write-channel skew: AW and W arrival offset in both orders with master-driven valid/ready timing
* Read-channel skew: AR issued with the slave R response held to exercise response-hold paths
* Protocol completion, response ordering, and final register contents preserved under skew

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the XTRIG AXI-Lite master in manual mode so AW/W/AR/B/R channel timing is driven explicitly.
. Apply write-channel skew.
a. Drive AW before W (and W before AW) with a configurable gap; hold the opposite channel valid-low to create ready-low windows.
b. Verify the write completes with a correct B response and the target CSR holds the written value.
. Apply read-channel skew.
a. Issue AR, then hold the R response by keeping the master RREADY low for several cycles (response-hold).
b. Verify ARVALID/ARADDR remain stable while ARREADY is low and the read returns correct data when released.
. Verify response ordering across the skewed accesses and that final XTRIG register contents match the reference model.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`xtrig_axi_skew_aw_w_chk` |AW/W skew in both orders completes with correct B response
|`xtrig_axi_skew_ready_low_chk` |Channels remain stable through ready-low / response-hold windows
|`xtrig_axi_skew_order_chk` |Response ordering is preserved across skewed accesses
|`xtrig_axi_skew_csr_chk` |Final XTRIG CSR contents match the programmed values
|===

*Pass Criteria*: All skewed AXI-Lite write and read accesses complete with correct responses, stable channels, preserved ordering, and matching CSR contents, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-69-dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_demux_aw_lock_release_test]]
===== 6.9 `dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_demux_aw_lock_release_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local XTRIG monitor

*Objective*: Demux AW-lock release

*Test Coverage*:

* Focused coverage of the XTRIG `axi_lite_demux` AW-lock release branch
* AW/W arrival skew that holds the demux AW lock, then releases channels in varied order
* Lock-release branch exercised after the locked write drains
* Response ordering and final CSR contents preserved across the locked/released sequence

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Configure the XTRIG AXI-Lite master in manual mode.
. Skew AW/W arrival to hold the demux AW lock.
a. Present AW (selecting a demux slave) while delaying W so the `axi_lite_demux` AW lock is held open.
b. Confirm via the XTRIG monitor that the AW lock is engaged.
. Release the channels in varied order (W-then-B, and across back-to-back writes) to exercise the AW-lock release branch.
a. Verify the lock releases once the locked write completes and the demux is free for the next AW.
. Verify response ordering and that final XTRIG CSR contents match the reference model.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`xtrig_demux_aw_lock_engage_chk` |AW/W skew engages the `axi_lite_demux` AW lock
|`xtrig_demux_aw_lock_release_chk` |The AW-lock release branch is exercised once the locked write completes
|`xtrig_demux_order_chk` |Response ordering is preserved across the locked/released writes
|`xtrig_demux_csr_chk` |Final XTRIG CSR contents match the programmed values
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The `axi_lite_demux` AW lock engages and releases correctly with preserved ordering and matching CSR contents, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-610-dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_read_decode_backpressure_test]]
===== 6.10 `dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_read_decode_backpressure_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_xtrig_axi_channel_skew_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local XTRIG monitor

*Objective*: Read decode backpressure

*Test Coverage*:

* Unmapped XTRIG reads routed to the xbar default DECERR slave (`+axi_lite_xbar.default_ar_mst_port[_en]+`)
* RREADY held low so a second ARVALID must stay stable while ARREADY is low (`axi_lite_demux.ar_valid_stable` / `ar_stable`)
* Both unmapped reads return DECERR; no deadlock on the backpressured read path
* Stable ARADDR and read data through the decode-backpressure window

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Idle all manual AXI-Lite master outputs after reset; confirm the XTRIG xbar address map has no valid slave at `0x02B0` and above.
. Issue an unmapped read to `0x02B0` and hold the DECERR response by keeping the master RREADY=0.
. While the first response is held, issue a second unmapped read to `0x02F0`.
a. Verify ARVALID remains asserted and ARADDR remains stable while ARREADY is low (read-stability assertions hit).
. Release RREADY, allow the second AR to complete, and verify the second response is also DECERR.
. Confirm via the XTRIG monitor that both reads returned DECERR with no deadlock and stable channel behavior throughout.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`xtrig_read_decerr_chk` |Both unmapped reads return DECERR from the xbar default slave
|`xtrig_read_ar_stable_chk` |ARVALID/ARADDR remain stable while ARREADY is low
|`xtrig_read_default_ar_chk` |The xbar `+default_ar_mst_port[_en]+` read path receives real success hits
|`xtrig_read_no_deadlock_chk` |The backpressured read path completes without deadlock
|===

*Pass Criteria*: Both unmapped reads return DECERR with stable channels through the backpressure window and the xbar default-read and demux read-stability assertions covered, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

NOTE: In the CTM entries that follow, `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` is
the register of output port `dst`, and each set bit names a source input port
(see the port-naming note at the head of this section).

[[sec-611-dtp_ctm_wire_or_cla_to_ctp_test]]
===== 6.11 `dtp_ctm_wire_or_cla_to_ctp_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_wire_or_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Wire-OR CLA to CTP

*Test Coverage*:

* CTM wire-OR (multicast) routing of one internal CLA source to multiple external CTP destinations
* `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` of every selected CTP output programmed with the single CLA source-input bit; the overlap step adds a second source-input bit to one of them
* All selected CTP destinations receive the trigger on the same source pulse; non-selected ports stay idle
* Wire-OR superposition when a second overlapping CLA source targets one of the same CTP destinations
* CSR readback of each selected output register matches its programmed source-input bits

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Program the multicast route via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`:
a. Write `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT = (1 << src)+` for each selected CTP output `dst` (e.g. outputs 0, 5, 12).
b. Read each written register back and confirm only the source-input bit is set.
. Drive a cross-trigger request on the CLA source via the OCAH-local CTM BFM.
. Observe the CTP destinations with the OCAH-local CTP monitor:
a. Verify every selected CTP destination asserts the trigger simultaneously.
b. Verify every non-selected destination port remains quiet.
. Exercise wire-OR superposition:
a. Program a second CLA source whose `CT_DST_SELECT` overlaps one of the same CTP destinations.
b. Pulse both sources and verify the overlapped destination ORs the contributions while disjoint destinations track only their own source.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_ctp_csr_chk` |CSR readback of each selected output's `CT_DST_SELECT` matches its programmed source-input bits
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_ctp_route_chk` |All selected CTP destinations assert on the CLA pulse against the reference model
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_ctp_or_chk` |Overlapping CLA sources OR together at the shared CTP destination
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_ctp_nospur_chk` |No non-selected destination port toggles
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The CLA source multicasts to exactly its selected CTP destinations, overlapping sources OR correctly, and no spurious destination toggles occur, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-612-dtp_ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_cla_test]]
===== 6.12 `dtp_ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_cla_test`

* All selected CLA destinations observe the trigger on the same CTP input pulse; non-selected stay idle
* Wire-OR superposition when an overlapping CTP source targets a shared CLA destination
* CSR readback of each selected output register matches its programmed source-input bits

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Program the multicast route via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`:
a. Write `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT = (1 << src)+` for each selected CLA output `dst`.
b. Read each written register back and confirm only the source-input bit is set.
. Drive a cross-trigger request on the external CTP source via the OCAH-local CTP BFM.
. Observe the CLA destinations with the OCAH-local CTM monitor:
a. Verify every selected CLA destination asserts simultaneously.
b. Verify non-selected CLA destinations remain quiet.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description

|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_cla_route_chk` |All selected CLA destinations assert on the CTP pulse against the reference model
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_cla_or_chk` |Overlapping CTP sources OR together at the shared CLA destination
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_cla_nospur_chk` |No non-selected CLA destination toggles
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The CTP source multicasts to exactly its selected CLA destinations, overlapping sources OR correctly, and no spurious destination toggles occur, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-613-dtp_ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_test]]
===== 6.13 `dtp_ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_wire_or_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Wire-OR CLA to CLA

*Test Coverage*:

* CTM wire-OR routing entirely between internal CLA channels (source CLA to multiple destination CLA)
* `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` of every selected CLA output programmed with the single CLA source-input bit
* All selected destination CLA channels pulse on the source CLA pulse; non-selected stay idle
* Wire-OR superposition of two CLA sources sharing a destination CLA channel
* No feedback loop where a destination CLA re-triggers its own source route

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Program the intra-CLA multicast route via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`:
a. Write `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT = (1 << src)+` for each selected CLA output `dst`.
b. Read each written register back and confirm only the source-input bit is set.
. Pulse the source CLA via the OCAH-local CTM BFM.
. Observe the destination CLA channels with the OCAH-local CTM monitor:
a. Verify every selected destination CLA pulses.
b. Verify the routing mask behavior: non-selected CLA channels stay idle and the source is not fed back to itself.
. Exercise wire-OR superposition with a second CLA source sharing one destination and confirm OR behavior on the shared channel.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_csr_chk` |CSR readback of each selected output's `CT_DST_SELECT` matches its programmed source-input bits
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_route_chk` |All selected destination CLA channels pulse against the reference model
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_or_chk` |Overlapping CLA sources OR together at the shared CLA destination
|`ctm_wire_or_cla_to_cla_noloop_chk` |No feedback loop or self-retrigger of the source CLA
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The CLA source multicasts to exactly its selected CLA destinations with correct wire-OR superposition and no feedback or spurious toggles, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-614-dtp_ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_test]]
===== 6.14 `dtp_ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_wire_or_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: Wire-OR CTP to CTP

*Test Coverage*:

* CTM wire-OR routing entirely between external CTP ports (source CTP to multiple destination CTP)
* `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` of every selected CTP output programmed with the single CTP source-input bit
* All selected destination CTP ports pulse with matching pulse width on the source CTP pulse
* Wire-OR superposition of two CTP sources sharing a destination CTP port
* No feedback loop from a destination CTP port back into the source route

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Program the inter-CTP multicast route via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`:
a. Write `+CT_SRC[dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT = (1 << src)+` for each selected CTP output `dst`.
b. Read each written register back and confirm only the source-input bit is set.
. Pulse the source CTP via the OCAH-local CTP BFM.
. Observe the destination CTP ports with the OCAH-local CTP monitor:
a. Verify every selected destination CTP port pulses.
b. Verify pulse width on each destination matches the reference model.
c. Verify no feedback loop re-triggers the source.
. Exercise wire-OR superposition with a second CTP source sharing one destination and confirm OR behavior.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_csr_chk` |CSR readback of each selected output's `CT_DST_SELECT` matches its programmed source-input bits
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_route_chk` |All selected destination CTP ports pulse against the reference model
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_width_chk` |Destination CTP pulse width matches the source pulse
|`ctm_wire_or_ctp_to_ctp_noloop_chk` |No feedback loop or self-retrigger of the source CTP
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The CTP source multicasts to exactly its selected CTP destinations with correct pulse width, wire-OR superposition, and no feedback or spurious toggles, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''

[[sec-615-dtp_ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_test]]
===== 6.15 `dtp_ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_test`

*Source Module*: `dtp_ctm_p2p_test`

*BFM / Model*: `ocah_axi_vip`, OCAH-local CTM/CTP BFMs

*Objective*: P2P CLA to CTP

*Test Coverage*:

* CTM point-to-point routing of an internal CLA source to a single external CTP destination
* `+CT_SRC[ctp_dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT+` programmed with exactly one source-input bit set
* Request appears only at the selected CTP destination; no spurious triggers elsewhere
* Several distinct CLA-source / CTP-destination pairs exercised to vary the single-bit position

*Test Procedure*:

[arabic]
. Program the point-to-point route via `OcahAxiLiteMasterAgent`:
a. Write `+CT_SRC[ctp_dst]_CONFIG_0.CT_DST_SELECT = (1 << src)+` so the destination CTP output selects the single source input.
b. Read the register back and confirm only the one source-input bit is set.
. Drive a cross-trigger request on the CLA source via the OCAH-local CTM BFM.
. Observe the destinations with the OCAH-local CTP monitor:
a. Verify the request appears at exactly the selected CTP destination.
b. Verify no other destination port observes the request.
. Repeat for several CLA-source / CTP-destination combinations to move the single set bit across the destination field.

*Checkers*:

[cols=",",options="header",]
|===
|Checker |Description
|`ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_csr_chk` |CSR readback of the destination CTP output register shows exactly one source-input bit set
|`ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_route_chk` |Trigger reaches exactly the selected CTP destination against the reference model
|`ctm_p2p_cla_to_ctp_nospur_chk` |No spurious triggers on non-selected destinations
|===

*Pass Criteria*: The CLA-to-CTP trigger is delivered only to the configured destination across all exercised pairs with no spurious activity, with zero scoreboard/reference-model errors.

'''''
